1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Diff-scoped floor checks for CONSTRAINTS.md (CR-01 to CR-04, CR-07, CR-08).
3	
4	Called by scripts/floor_guard.sh, which writes the diff, the untracked-file list and the
5	base commit's pyproject.toml. Prints one line per finding and exits 1 if there are any:
6	
7	    path:line: [rule] message
8	
9	Rules (CONSTRAINTS.md ID in brackets):
10	    suppression [CR-01]            a new `type: ignore`, `pyright:`, `noqa`, `pragma: no cover`...
11	    stub [CR-02]                   a function whose body is only pass, ... or NotImplementedError
12	    stub-task [CR-02]              an interface stub, `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`, naming
13	                                   a task that is not open in tasks/todo.md (whole repo, not the diff)
14	    stub-left [CR-02]              any interface stub, with --exit (the milestone exit)
15	    swallowed-exception [CR-02]    `except:` / `except Exception:` with only pass
16	    test-skipped [CR-03]           a new skip, skipif, xfail or importorskip
17	    test-deleted [CR-03]           a test file removed
18	    assertion-removed [CR-03]      a test file lost more assertion lines than it gained
19	    test-without-assertion [CR-03] a changed test function that asserts nothing
20	    secret [CR-04]                 a credential pattern (the value is never printed)
21	    references-import [CR-08]      an import from the references folder
22	    references-path [CR-08]        the references folder put on an import path (sys.path,
23	                                   an environment variable, pytest or type-checker config)
24	    probes-import, probes-path     the same for the probes folder [CR-08]
25	    bar-loosened [CR-07]           pyproject.toml tool config weakened, or a new config file
26	    rule-removed [CR-07]           a CR rule gone from CONSTRAINTS.md
27	    new-exception [CR-07]          a new row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table
28	
29	An interface stub is the one placeholder body CR-02 allows: the milestone's interface task writes
30	each step signature that way, naming the task that replaces it, and testers import it before the
31	code exists. It is never a `stub` finding; `stub-task` and `stub-left` judge it instead.
32	
33	A CR-01 to CR-03 or CR-08 finding is allowed when an unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's
34	Exceptions table names that rule and a path glob matching the file. Secrets and CR-07 never are.
35	Files under scripts/ are skipped for code checks: they are the bar, protected separately.
36	Files under references/ and probes/ are inputs, not project code: only the secret check reads them.
37	"""

99	TASK_ID = re.compile(r"M\d+-T\d{2,}")
100	OPEN_TASK = re.compile(r"^\s*- \[ \] (M\d+-T\d{2,})\b", re.MULTILINE)

105	class Finding(NamedTuple):
106	    rule: str
107	    path: str
108	    line: int  # 0 when the finding has no single line
109	    message: str
110	
111	    def __str__(self) -> str:
112	        where = f"{self.path}:{self.line}" if self.line else self.path
113	        return f"{where}: [{self.rule}] {self.message}"

116	@dataclass
117	class Diff:
118	    added: dict[str, list[tuple[int, str]]] = field(default_factory=dict)
119	    removed: dict[str, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
120	    deleted: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
121	
122	    def added_lines(self, path: str) -> set[int]:
123	        return {n for n, _ in self.added.get(path, [])}
124	
125	
126	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- the diff
127	HUNK = re.compile(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@")
128	
129	
130	def _diff_path(raw: str) -> str:
131	    raw = raw.rstrip("\t")
132	    return raw[2:] if raw[:2] in {"a/", "b/"} else raw
133	
134	
135	def parse_diff(text: str) -> Diff:
136	    """Added lines (with new line numbers), removed lines and deleted files, per file."""
137	    diff = Diff()
138	    old = new = ""
139	    line_no = 0
140	    in_header = False
141	    for line in text.splitlines():
142	        if line.startswith("diff --git "):
143	            old, new, in_header = "", "", True
144	        elif in_header and line.startswith("--- "):
145	            old = _diff_path(line[4:])
146	        elif in_header and line.startswith("+++ "):
147	            new = _diff_path(line[4:])
148	            if new == "/dev/null":
149	                diff.deleted.add(old)
150	        elif match := HUNK.match(line):
151	            in_header, line_no = False, int(match[1])
152	        elif in_header:
153	            continue
154	        elif line.startswith("+"):
155	            diff.added.setdefault(new, []).append((line_no, line[1:]))
156	            line_no += 1
157	        elif line.startswith("-"):
158	            diff.removed.setdefault(old, []).append(line[1:])
159	    return diff
160	
161	
162	def add_untracked(diff: Diff, paths: list[str]) -> None:
163	    """An untracked file is all added lines."""
164	    for name in paths:
165	        path = Path(name)
166	        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
167	            continue
168	        try:
169	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
170	        except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
171	            continue
172	        diff.added[name] = list(enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1))
173	
174	
175	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- line checks
176	def is_outside(path: str) -> bool:
177	    return path.startswith(tuple(f"{folder}/" for folder in OUTSIDE))
178	
179	
180	def is_python(path: str) -> bool:
181	    if path.startswith("scripts/") or is_outside(path):
182	        return False
183	    return path.endswith((".py", ".pyi"))
184	
185	
186	def is_test_file(path: str) -> bool:
187	    parts = Path(path).parts
188	    name = parts[-1] if parts else ""
189	    return (
190	        "tests" in parts
191	        and (name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py"))
192	        and not is_outside(path)
193	    )
194	
195	
196	def line_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
197	    found: list[Finding] = []
198	    for path, lines in diff.added.items():
199	        python, test = is_python(path), is_test_file(path)
200	        path_settings = not is_outside(path) and not path.endswith(DOC_SUFFIXES)
201	        for n, text in lines:
202	            if python and SUPPRESSION.search(text):
203	                found.append(Finding("suppression", path, n, "new suppression comment"))
204	            if path_settings and IMPORT_PATH_SETTING.search(text):
205	                found += [
206	                    Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, n, f"{folder}/ on an import path")
207	                    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items()
208	                    if word.search(text)
209	                ]
210	            if test and SKIP.search(text):
211	                found.append(Finding("test-skipped", path, n, "test skipped or marked xfail"))
212	            if Path(path).name not in LOCKFILES:
213	                found += [
214	                    Finding("secret", path, n, f"looks like a {kind} (value not shown)")
215	                    for kind, pattern in SECRETS.items()
216	                    if pattern.search(text)
217	                ]
218	    return found
219	
220	
221	def test_file_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
222	    found = [
223	        Finding("test-deleted", p, 0, "test file deleted") for p in diff.deleted if is_test_file(p)
224	    ]
225	    for path, lines in diff.removed.items():
226	        if not is_test_file(path) or path in diff.deleted:
227	            continue
228	        lost = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for t in lines)
229	        gained = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []))
230	        if lost > gained:
231	            found.append(
232	                Finding("assertion-removed", path, 0, f"{lost - gained} assertion line(s) removed")
233	            )
234	    return found
235

237	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- AST checks
238	def _touches(node: ast.AST, lines: set[int]) -> bool:
239	    start = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
240	    end = getattr(node, "end_lineno", start) or start
241	    return any(start <= n <= end for n in lines)
242	
243	
244	def _is_trivial(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> bool:
245	    """Only pass, `...` or `raise NotImplementedError`, after an optional docstring."""
246	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
247	    return all(_is_placeholder(s) for s in stmts)
248	
249	
250	def _task_stub(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> str | None:
251	    """The task an interface stub names: a body that is only `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`."""
252	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
253	    if len(stmts) != 1 or not isinstance(stmts[0], ast.Raise):
254	        return None
255	    exc = stmts[0].exc
256	    if not (isinstance(exc, ast.Call) and _name(exc.func) == "NotImplementedError"):
257	        return None
258	    arg = exc.args[0] if len(exc.args) == 1 and not exc.keywords else None
259	    if (
260	        isinstance(arg, ast.Constant)
261	        and isinstance(arg.value, str)
262	        and TASK_ID.fullmatch(arg.value)
263	    ):
264	        return arg.value
265	    return None
266	
267	
268	def _is_docstring(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
269	    return (
270	        isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr)
271	        and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant)
272	        and isinstance(stmt.value.value, str)
273	    )
274	
275	
276	def _is_placeholder(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
277	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass):
278	        return True
279	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant):
280	        return stmt.value.value is Ellipsis
281	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Raise) and stmt.exc is not None:
282	        exc = stmt.exc.func if isinstance(stmt.exc, ast.Call) else stmt.exc
283	        return _name(exc) == "NotImplementedError"
284	    return False
285	
286	
287	def _name(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
288	    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
289	        return node.id
290	    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
291	        return node.attr
292	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
293	        return _name(node.func)
294	    return None
295	
296	
297	def _exempt_functions(tree: ast.Module) -> set[ast.AST]:
298	    """Abstract methods, overloads and Protocol members may have placeholder bodies."""
299	    exempt: set[ast.AST] = set()
300	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
301	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
302	            if {_name(d) for d in node.decorator_list} & EXEMPT_DECORATORS:
303	                exempt.add(node)
304	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) and "Protocol" in {_name(b) for b in node.bases}:
305	            exempt.update(
306	                n for n in node.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
307	            )
308	    return exempt
309	
310	
311	def _in_folder(name: str | None, folder: str) -> bool:
312	    return name is not None and (name == folder or name.startswith(f"{folder}."))
313	
314	
315	def _mentions(node: ast.AST, word: re.Pattern[str]) -> bool:
316	    return any(
317	        isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value, str) and word.search(n.value)
318	        for n in ast.walk(node)
319	    )
320	
321	
322	def _is_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
323	    return (
324	        isinstance(node, ast.Attribute)
325	        and node.attr == "path"
326	        and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
327	        and node.value.id == "sys"
328	    )
329	
330	
331	def _imported_modules(node: ast.AST) -> list[str | None]:
332	    """Absolute module names an import statement or an import_module call brings in."""
333	    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
334	        return [alias.name for alias in node.names]
335	    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
336	        return [node.module] if node.level == 0 else []
337	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and _name(node.func) in {"import_module", "__import__"}:
338	        first = node.args[0] if node.args else None
339	        if isinstance(first, ast.Constant) and isinstance(first.value, str):
340	            return [first.value]
341	    return []
342	
343	
344	def _changes_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
345	    """sys.path.insert/append/extend, site.addsitedir, or an assignment to sys.path."""
346	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
347	        func = node.func
348	        return func.attr == "addsitedir" or (
349	            func.attr in SYS_PATH_CALLS and _is_sys_path(func.value)
350	        )
351	    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
352	        return any(_is_sys_path(target) for target in node.targets)
353	    if isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
354	        return _is_sys_path(node.target)
355	    return False
356	
357	
358	def _outside_finding(path: str, node: ast.AST) -> Finding | None:
359	    """CR-08: an import from references/ or probes/, or either folder added to sys.path."""
360	    line = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
361	    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items():
362	        if any(_in_folder(name, folder) for name in _imported_modules(node)):
363	            return Finding(f"{folder}-import", path, line, f"import from {folder}/")
364	        if _changes_sys_path(node) and _mentions(node, word):
365	            return Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, line, f"{folder}/ added to sys.path")
366	    return None
367	
368	
369	def _asserts(func: ast.AST) -> bool:
370	    for node in ast.walk(func):
371	        if isinstance(node, ast.Assert):
372	            return True
373	        name = _name(node) if isinstance(node, ast.Call) else None
374	        if name and (
375	            name.startswith("assert") or name in {"raises", "warns", "deprecated_call", "fail"}
376	        ):
377	            return True
378	    return False
379	
380	
381	def ast_findings(path: str, lines: set[int]) -> list[Finding]:
382	    try:
383	        tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
384	    except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
385	        return []  # ruff reports syntax errors; a deleted file has nothing to check
386	    exempt, test = _exempt_functions(tree), is_test_file(path)
387	    found: list[Finding] = []
388	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
389	        if not _touches(node, lines):
390	            continue
391	        if outside := _outside_finding(path, node):
392	            found.append(outside)
393	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
394	            if (
395	                node not in exempt
396	                and not path.endswith(".pyi")
397	                and _is_trivial(node.body)
398	                and not _task_stub(node.body)
399	            ):
400	                found.append(
401	                    Finding("stub", path, node.lineno, f"`{node.name}` has a placeholder body")
402	                )
403	            if test and node.name.startswith("test") and not _asserts(node):
404	                found.append(
405	                    Finding(
406	                        "test-without-assertion",
407	                        path,
408	                        node.lineno,
409	                        f"`{node.name}` asserts nothing",
410	                    )
411	                )
412	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and _is_trivial(node.body):
413	            if (None if node.type is None else _name(node.type)) in TRIVIAL_EXCEPT:
414	                found.append(
415	                    Finding("swallowed-exception", path, node.lineno, "exception swallowed")
416	                )
417	    return found
418	
419	
420	def open_tasks() -> set[str]:
421	    """Task IDs with an unchecked line in tasks/todo.md (none when there is no todo)."""
422	    path = Path("tasks/todo.md")
423	    return set(OPEN_TASK.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))) if path.is_file() else set()
424	
425	
426	def task_stub_findings(paths: list[str], at_exit: bool) -> list[Finding]:
427	    """Interface stubs anywhere in the project's code: each must name an open task, and at the
428	    milestone exit none may be left. Not diff-scoped: the stub was written by an earlier task."""
429	    open_ids, found = open_tasks(), []
430	    for path in paths:
431	        if not is_python(path) or is_test_file(path) or not Path(path).is_file():
432	            continue
433	        try:
434	            tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
435	        except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
436	            continue
437	        for node in ast.walk(tree):
438	            if not isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
439	                continue
440	            if not (task := _task_stub(node.body)):
441	                continue
442	            if at_exit:
443	                found.append(
444	                    Finding(
445	                        "stub-left",
446	                        path,
447	                        node.lineno,
448	                        f"`{node.name}` is still the stub for {task}",
449	                    )
450	                )
451	            elif task not in open_ids:
452	                found.append(
453	                    Finding(
454	                        "stub-task",
455	                        path,
456	                        node.lineno,
457	                        f"`{node.name}` is a stub naming {task}, which is not open in tasks/todo.md",
458	                    )
459	                )
460	    return found

466	    "tool.size_limits.max_module_lines",

472	TRUE_KEYS = ("tool.mypy.strict", "tool.pytest.ini_options.xfail_strict")
473	OFF_KEYS = ("tool.pylint.main.exit-zero",)  # turning one on silences a check
474	NO_SHRINK = ("tool.ruff.lint.select", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-select")
475	NO_GROW = (
476	    "tool.ruff.lint.ignore", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-ignore", "tool.ruff.exclude",
477	    "tool.ruff.extend-exclude", "tool.ruff.lint.exclude", "tool.deptry.exclude",
478	    "tool.deptry.extend_exclude", "tool.deptry.ignore", "tool.vulture.ignore_names",
479	    "tool.vulture.exclude", "tool.mypy.exclude", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-paths",
480	    "tool.pylint.main.ignore", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-patterns",
481	)  # fmt: skip
482	# Excluding the references or probes folder is not a loosening: neither is project code (CR-08).
483	OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE = re.compile(r"\^?(references|probes)/?\*{0,2}")
484	NO_GROW_TABLES = ("tool.ruff.lint.per-file-ignores", "tool.deptry.per_rule_ignores")
485	MUST_MATCH = ("tool.importlinter", "tool.mypy.overrides", "tool.pyright")
486	STRICT_FLAGS = ("--strict-markers", "--strict-config")
487	
488	
489	def _get(data: dict[str, Any], dotted: str) -> Any:
490	    for key in dotted.split("."):
491	        if not isinstance(data, dict) or key not in data:
492	            return None
493	        data = data[key]
494	    return data
495	
496	
497	def _as_set(value: Any) -> set[str]:
498	    if isinstance(value, str):
499	        return {value}
500	    return {str(v) for v in value} if isinstance(value, list) else set()
501	
502	
503	def _limit_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
504	    pylint = _get(old, "tool.ruff.lint.pylint") or {}
505	    maxes = [*MAX_KEYS, *(f"tool.ruff.lint.pylint.{k}" for k in pylint)]
506	    out = [
507	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
508	        for k in maxes
509	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), +1)
510	    ]
511	    out += [
512	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
513	        for k in MIN_KEYS
514	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), -1)
515	    ]
516	    out += [
517	        f"{k} turned off" for k in TRUE_KEYS if _get(old, k) is True and _get(new, k) is not True
518	    ]
519	    out += [f"{k} turned on" for k in OFF_KEYS if _get(new, k) is True and _get(old, k) is not True]
520	    return out
521	
522	
523	def _loosened(was: Any, now: Any, direction: int) -> bool:
524	    if not isinstance(was, (int, float)):
525	        return False
526	    if not isinstance(now, (int, float)):
527	        return True  # a limit that disappeared
528	    return (now - was) * direction > 0
529	
530	
531	def _list_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
532	    out = [f"{k} lost {sorted(_as_set(_get(old, k)) - _as_set(_get(new, k)))}" for k in NO_SHRINK]
533	    for k in NO_GROW:
534	        gained = _as_set(_get(new, k)) - _as_set(_get(old, k))
535	        out.append(f"{k} gained {sorted(g for g in gained if not OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE.fullmatch(g))}")
536	    for table in NO_GROW_TABLES:
537	        was, now = _get(old, table) or {}, _get(new, table) or {}
538	        out += [
539	            f"{table}.{key} gained {sorted(_as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key)))}"
540	            for key, codes in now.items()
541	            if _as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key))
542	        ]
543	    flags = _as_set((_get(new, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts") or "").split())
544	    out += [
545	        f"pytest addopts dropped {f}"
546	        for f in STRICT_FLAGS
547	        if f in str(_get(old, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts")) and f not in flags
548	    ]
549	    out += [
550	        f"{k} changed (boundaries and type config change only through build-setup)"
551	        for k in MUST_MATCH
552	        if _get(old, k) != _get(new, k)
553	    ]
554	    return [o for o in out if not o.endswith("[]")]
555	
556	
557	def config_findings(old_pyproject: Path, diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
558	    found = [
559	        Finding("bar-loosened", p, 0, "new tool config file overrides pyproject.toml")
560	        for p in diff.added
561	        if Path(p).name in CONFIG_FILES and not p.startswith("scripts/")
562	    ]
563	    old_text = old_pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if old_pyproject.is_file() else ""
564	    if not old_text or not Path("pyproject.toml").is_file():
565	        return found
566	    try:
567	        old, new = (
568	            tomllib.loads(old_text),
569	            tomllib.loads(Path("pyproject.toml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")),
570	        )
571	    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as exc:
572	        return [*found, Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, f"cannot parse: {exc}")]
573	    changes = _limit_findings(old, new) + _list_findings(old, new)
574	    return found + [Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, c) for c in changes]
575	
576	
577	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- CONSTRAINTS.md
578	RULE_REF = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:-\s*\*\*(CR-\d+)\*\*|\|\s*(CR-\d+)\s*\|)")
579	
580	
581	def _section(lines: list[str], title: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
582	    """1-based line range of a `## title` section, or (0, -1)."""
583	    start = next((i for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if t.strip().lower() == f"## {title}"), 0)
584	    if not start:
585	        return 0, -1
586	    end = next(
587	        (i - 1 for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if i > start and t.startswith("## ")), len(lines)
588	    )
589	    return start, end
590	
591	
592	def _table_rows(lines: list[str], start: int, end: int) -> list[tuple[int, list[str]]]:
593	    rows = []
594	    for n in range(start + 1, end + 1):
595	        text = lines[n - 1].strip()
596	        if not text.startswith("|"):
597	            continue
598	        cells = [c.strip() for c in text.strip("|").split("|")]
599	        if cells and cells[0] not in {"", "ID"} and not set(cells[0]) <= set("-: "):
600	            rows.append((n, cells))
601	    return rows
602	
603	
604	def constraints_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
605	    path = "CONSTRAINTS.md"
606	    if path not in diff.added and path not in diff.removed:
607	        return []
608	    still = {
609	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
610	    }
611	    gone = {
612	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for t in diff.removed.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
613	    }
614	    found = [Finding("rule-removed", path, 0, f"{rule} removed") for rule in sorted(gone - still)]
615	    lines = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if Path(path).is_file() else []
616	    start, end = _section(lines, "exceptions")
617	    new_rows = diff.added_lines(path)
618	    found += [
619	        Finding("new-exception", path, n, f"new exception {cells[0]} needs the user's yes")
620	        for n, cells in _table_rows(lines, start, end)
621	        if n in new_rows
622	    ]
623	    return found
624	
625	
626	def load_exceptions(today: date) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
627	    """(rule cell, path glob) for each unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table."""
628	    path = Path("CONSTRAINTS.md")
629	    if not path.is_file():
630	        return []
631	    lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
632	    allowed = []
633	    for _, cells in _table_rows(lines, *_section(lines, "exceptions")):
634	        if len(cells) < 6:
635	            continue
636	        expires = re.match(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", cells[5])
637	        if expires and date.fromisoformat(expires[0]) < today:
638	            continue
639	        allowed.append((cells[1], cells[2].strip("`")))
640	    return allowed
641	
642	
643	def is_excepted(finding: Finding, allowed: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> bool:
644	    rule_id = RULE_IDS.get(finding.rule)
645	    return bool(rule_id) and any(
646	        rule_id in rule and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding.path, glob) for rule, glob in allowed
647	    )
648	
649	
650	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- main
651	def run(
652	    diff: Diff, old_pyproject: Path, python_files: list[str] | None = None, at_exit: bool = False
653	) -> list[Finding]:
654	    found = line_findings(diff) + test_file_findings(diff)
655	    found += task_stub_findings(python_files or [], at_exit)
656	    for path in diff.added:
657	        if is_python(path):
658	            found += ast_findings(path, diff.added_lines(path))
659	    found += config_findings(old_pyproject, diff) + constraints_findings(diff)
660	    allowed = load_exceptions(datetime.now(tz=UTC).date())
661	    return [f for f in found if not is_excepted(f, allowed)]
662	
663	
664	def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
665	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
666	    parser.add_argument("--diff", type=Path, required=True)
667	    parser.add_argument("--untracked", type=Path, required=True)
668	    parser.add_argument("--old-pyproject", type=Path, required=True)
669	    parser.add_argument(
670	        "--python", type=Path, help="every Python file in the project, one per line"
671	    )
672	    parser.add_argument(
673	        "--exit", action="store_true", help="the milestone exit: no stub may be left"
674	    )
675	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
676	    diff = parse_diff(args.diff.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
677	    add_untracked(diff, args.untracked.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines())
678	    python_files = args.python.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if args.python else []
679	    findings = run(diff, args.old_pyproject, python_files, args.exit)
680	    if not findings:
681	        print("floor_guard: clean", file=sys.stderr)
682	        return 0
683	    print("\n".join(str(f) for f in sorted(findings)))
684	    print(
685	        f"floor_guard: {len(findings)} floor violation(s). Fix the code; a real exception goes "
686	        "through build-setup (Revise) and the Exceptions table in CONSTRAINTS.md.",
687	        file=sys.stderr,
688	    )
689	    return 1
690	
691	
692	if __name__ == "__main__":
693	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

121	
122	def contract_modules(repo: Repo, pkg: str) -> list[str]:
123	    """The files that make up a package's contract: each component's `__init__.py` (single package), else
124	    `src/**/<pkg>/__init__.py`. Repo-relative."""
125	    inits = [f for f in py_files(repo.root / "src") if f.name == "__init__.py"]
126	    names = [c.id for c in package_components(repo, pkg)] if not is_multi(repo) else []
127	    out = [f for f in inits if f.parent.name in names and pkg in f.parts]
128	    if not out:
129	        out = [f for f in inits if f.parent.name == pkg]
130	    return sorted(str(f.relative_to(repo.root)) for f in out)
131	
132	
133	def stale_contracts(repo: Repo) -> list[str]:
134	    """Packages whose contract code was committed after their contract.md."""
135	    if not git.is_repo(repo.root):
136	        return []
137	    out = []
138	    for pkg in known_packages(repo):
139	        doc = repo.package_doc(pkg, "contract")
140	        modules = contract_modules(repo, pkg)
141	        if not doc or not modules:
142	            continue
143	        doc_t = git.last_commit_time(repo.root, doc.rel)
144	        code_t = git.last_commit_time(repo.root, *modules)
145	        if doc_t and code_t and code_t > doc_t:
146	            day = datetime.date.fromtimestamp
147	            out.append(f"{pkg} (contract.md {day(doc_t)}, code {day(code_t)})")
148	    return out
149	
150	
151	def drift(repo: Repo, pipelines: list[model.Pipeline]) -> list[str] | None:
152	    """`dt layout`'s drift line per drifting pipeline, or None when drift can't be computed yet."""
153	    from .. import code
154	    out = []
155	    for p in pipelines:
156	        try:
157	            res = code.pipeline_drift(repo, p)
158	        except NotImplementedError:
159	            return None
160	        except Exception as e:   # a parse failure in project code must not break status
161	            out.append(f"{p.id}: unknown ({type(e).__name__})")
162	            continue
163	        if res:
164	            out.append(res if res.startswith(p.id) else f"{p.id}: {res}")
165	    return out
166

33:- **WORLD-01** A world starts a new episode from a seed, and the same seed always gives the same starting state.
35:- **WORLD-02** Given the same seed and the same sequence of actions, a world produces the same sequence of observations, rewards and end signals.
37:- **WORLD-03** Each agent's observation is a fixed-length list of numbers, never an image.
39:- **WORLD-04** A world declares its agents, the observation length and the action format (a choice from a fixed set, or a list of numbers within stated limits) before any episode starts.
41:- **WORLD-05** Every world reports a per-episode score with a stated direction (higher is better, or lower is better), used by evaluation and charts.
43:- **WORLD-06** A world reports its drawable state at every step (positions, sizes and kinds of every object) for the replay viewer.
45:- **WORLD-07** A world can hold more than one agent, each agent belonging to a named team.
62:- **TRAIN-03** A run saves a checkpoint at every 10% of its budget, from the start (untrained) to the end.
64:- **TRAIN-04** A run evaluates its current agent at every 5% of its budget on 20 episodes whose seeds are never used in training.
84:- **LOG-01** Every run records its world, variant, method settings, seed, budget, start and end times, and whether it finished.
86:- **LOG-02** Every run logs, for each training episode, the episode's score, length and total reward per team.
143:- **EVAL-05** "Clearly beats" also needs every single run to beat the rule baseline: each run's final mean score is better than the rule-based baseline's mean score.
145:- **EVAL-06** Final evaluation produces one summary per world and variant: mean and range of learned scores across runs, both baseline means, the EVAL-04 test results and the EVAL-05 verdict.
190:- **SNAKE-01** The world is a 10 × 10 grid with one snake starting 3 cells long in the middle, and one food item on a random free cell.
192:- **SNAKE-02** Each step the snake chooses one of three actions: turn left, turn right or go straight.
194:- **SNAKE-03** Eating food grows the snake by one cell, adds 1 to the score and places new food on a random free cell.
196:- **SNAKE-04** The episode ends when the head hits a wall or the snake's body, or after 100 steps without eating.
198:- **SNAKE-05** The score is the food eaten in the episode (higher is better).
200:- **SNAKE-06** The snake observes a fixed list of numbers: danger straight ahead, left and right; its heading; the food's direction relative to its head; and its length.
202:- **SNAKE-07** The rule-based baseline moves toward the food along the shorter axis, unless that move would end the episode next step, in which case it takes any move that does not.
204:- **SNAKE-08** The trained snake beats both baselines as in EVAL-04 and EVAL-05.
235:- **FISH-10** Every variant's trained fish beat both baselines as in EVAL-04 and EVAL-05.
264:- **PREY-08** Each trained team is evaluated against fixed opponents: trained prey against the rule-based predators, compared with random and rule-based prey; trained predators against the rule-based prey, compared with random and rule-based predators. Each passes as in EVAL-04 and EVAL-05.

U01.S34Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/src/rllab/core/types.py (194 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/src/rllab/core/types.py
"""Shared types: the world and policy protocols, specs, steps, episodes and outputs.

`MethodConfig` and `RunConfig` are defined here too, though `methods` and `train` export them:
`runlog` records both (`RunRecord.config`, `Checkpoint.methods`) and may import only `core`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray

Direction = Literal["higher", "lower"]
Settings = Mapping[str, int | float | str | bool]
Array = NDArray[Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Discrete:
    """Actions are ints in `[0, n)`."""

    n: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Box:
    """Actions are `dims` floats in `[low, high]`."""

    dims: int
    low: float
    high: float


ActionSpace = Discrete | Box


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    """A named team: its agents' indices, its score's name and direction."""

    name: str
    agents: tuple[int, ...]
    score_name: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MeasureSpec:
    """An extra per-episode measure a world reports for one team."""

    name: str
    team: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WorldSpec:
    """Everything a world declares before any episode starts (WORLD-04)."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    agents: tuple[str, ...]
    teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]
    obs_len: int
    action: ActionSpace
    max_steps: int
    measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]
    sight: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Step:
    """One world step for every agent: float32 obs and rewards, a bool active mask."""

    obs: Array
    rewards: Array
    active: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EpisodeResult:
    """One episode's scores and total rewards per team, and its measures."""

    seed: int
    length: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Drawable:
    """One object on screen; `agent` is set for agents."""

    kind: str
    x: float
    y: float
    size: float
    team: str | None
    agent: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DrawState:
    """Every object on screen at one step (WORLD-06)."""

    width: float
    height: float
    objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Frame:
    """One traced step: what to draw, running team scores, latest rewards per agent."""

    step: int
    draw: DrawState
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    rewards: Array


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Episode:
    """A played episode; `frames` only when traced, `result.length + 1` of them."""

    result: EpisodeResult
    frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None
    hit_step_limit: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Output:
    """A written chart, video or write-up, and the runs, checkpoints and seeds behind it."""

    path: Path
    kind: Literal["chart", "video", "writeup"]
    made_from: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MethodConfig:
    """A learning method and its full settings (exported as `methods.MethodConfig`)."""

    method: Literal["dqn", "ppo"]
    settings: Settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunConfig:
    """One training run's settings (exported as `train.RunConfig`)."""

    name: str
    world: str
    variant: str
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    seed: int
    budget: int
    check_settings: Mapping[str, Settings]
    device: str = "cpu"


class ActionError(ValueError):
    """An action outside the world's declared action format; never clipped."""

    def __init__(self, agent: str, value: object) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"action {value!r} for agent {agent!r} is outside the action space")
        self.agent = agent
        self.value = value


class World(Protocol):
    """A seeded multi-agent world (AD-04)."""

    spec: WorldSpec

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...

    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...

    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...

    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...


class Policy(Protocol):
    """Acts for the `k` agents of one team, deterministically for a given obs and rng state."""

    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...
full input and output

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from rllab.core import Policy


class RulePolicy(Policy, Protocol):
    """A world's hand-written rule, acting on observations only (EVAL-02)."""

    description: str

class BaselineMeans:
    """Random and rule mean scores per team over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    random: Mapping[str, float]
    rule: Mapping[str, float]

"""Method types: configs, transitions, the learner protocol and its errors."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Array, Policy
from rllab.core.types import MethodConfig

__all__ = ["Learner", "MethodConfig", "NonFiniteError", "SpecMismatchError", "Transition"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Transition:
    """One world step for a team's agents, rows in `TeamSpec.agents` order."""

    obs: Array
    actions: Array
    rewards: Array
    next_obs: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool
    active: Array


class Learner(Protocol):
    """A hand-written learner for one team (CHECK-01)."""

    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...

    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...

    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...

    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...


class NonFiniteError(ValueError):
    """A loss or parameter became NaN or infinite; raised before any parameter changes."""

    def __init__(self, value: float) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"non-finite value {value!r} in a loss or parameter")
        self.value = value


class SpecMismatchError(ValueError):
    """A checkpoint's world spec differs from the world's, in the named fields."""

    def __init__(self, fields: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"checkpoint spec differs from the world's in: {', '.join(fields)}")
        self.fields = fields

"""Builds learners from configs and frozen policies from checkpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Policy, Settings, WorldSpec
from rllab.methods.dqn.settings import DEFAULTS as DQN_DEFAULTS
from rllab.methods.types import Learner, MethodConfig
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint

_DEFAULTS: dict[str, Settings] = {"dqn": DQN_DEFAULTS}


def method_defaults(method: str) -> Settings:
    """Every setting `method` declares, at its default value."""
    if method not in _DEFAULTS:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown method {method!r}; known: {', '.join(sorted(_DEFAULTS))}")
    return _DEFAULTS[method]


def make_learner(
    config: MethodConfig, spec: WorldSpec, team: str, rng: np.random.Generator
) -> Learner:
    """`dqn` for a `Discrete` action space, `ppo` for a `Box` one."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")


def load_policy(ckpt: Checkpoint, team: str, spec: WorldSpec) -> Policy:
    """The frozen policy `team` had at `ckpt`; `SpecMismatchError` if the specs differ."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/src/rllab/runlog/types.py
"""Run-folder types: the run record, evaluation points, checkpoints and the read-only run view."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, WorldSpec
from rllab.core.types import MethodConfig, RunConfig


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Failure:
    """Where a failed run stopped: the step, the non-finite value, the last good checkpoint."""

    step: int
    value: float
    checkpoint: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    """The LOG-01 record of one run; `config.methods` holds full settings."""

    config: RunConfig
    spec: WorldSpec
    started: datetime
    ended: datetime | None
    status: Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]
    failure: Failure | None
    path: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalPoint:
    """Means over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes at one evaluation step."""

    step: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Checkpoint:
    """A run's learners at `pct` of the budget, with the spec and method configs to rebuild them."""

    run: str
    pct: int
    step: int
    spec: WorldSpec
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]
    path: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Progress:
    """One snapshot of a run, at most one evaluation point behind it (LOG-06)."""

    step: int
    budget: int
    latest: EvalPoint | None
    elapsed: timedelta
    status: str


class Run(Protocol):
    """A read-only view of one run folder, readable while the run writes."""

    record: RunRecord

    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...

    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...

    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...


class RunExistsError(FileExistsError):
    """A run folder with this name already exists; nothing was written."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"run folder {path} already exists")
        self.path = path

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, WorldSpec
from rllab.core.types import RunConfig
from rllab.runlog.types import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Failure, RunRecord


class RunWriter:
    """Creates `root/<world>/<variant>/<name>`; `RunExistsError` if it exists."""

    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None:
        """Appends the point and flushes every log."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

"""Training types: the run config, the method check's result and its refusal."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from rllab.core.types import RunConfig

__all__ = ["CheckFailedError", "CheckResult", "RunConfig"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CheckResult:
    """A method's agreement with the check world's best actions; passed at >= 0.95."""

    method: str
    world: str
    agreement: float
    passed: bool


class CheckFailedError(RuntimeError):
    """A method failed its check world, so the batch was refused before any run started."""

    def __init__(self, result: CheckResult) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            f"method check failed: {result.method} on {result.world} agreed "
            f"{result.agreement:.0%} with the best actions (needs 95%)"
        )
        self.result = result

"""Final-evaluation types: comparisons, verdicts and per-team summaries."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Comparison:
    """One learned-vs-baseline test; `difference` > 0 means better in the team's direction."""

    baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]
    team: str
    learned_mean: float
    baseline_mean: float
    difference: float
    p_raw: float
    p_holm: float
    rejected: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Verdict:
    """EVAL-04 and EVAL-05: both nulls rejected and every run beating the rule mean."""

    beats_baselines: bool
    failing_runs: tuple[str, ...]
    runs_needed: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Summary:
    """One world, variant and team's final evaluation (EVAL-06)."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    team: str
    runs: tuple[str, ...]
    learned_mean: float
    learned_range: tuple[float, float]
    run_means: Mapping[str, float]
    random_mean: float
    rule_mean: float
    comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]
    verdict: Verdict
    collapse: int | None

"""The paired permutation test and the Holm correction (EVAL-04), per `null-hypothesis-testing`."""

ALPHA = 0.05

    learned: Array,
    baseline: Array,
    direction: Direction,
    *,
    permutations: int = 10_000,
    rng: np.random.Generator,
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """(difference, one-sided p) of learned (runs x seeds) against baseline (one per seed)."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T25")

    """Holm-adjusted p-values, in the order given; reject where adjusted p <= `ALPHA`."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T25")

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/src/rllab/cli/__main__.py
"""The `rllab` command: parses arguments, calls each trigger's pipeline entry, prints the result.

`cli` owns no pipeline step (AD-10). Every error prints as one line naming its cause, exit code 1.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl04_watch import run_watch
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl05_chart import run_chart
from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl06_evaluate import run_evaluate
from rllab.train import run_batch, run_check


def parse_seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """`1-5` or `1,3,7` (or a mix) as a list of seeds."""
    seeds: list[int] = []
    for part in text.split(","):
        first, _, last = part.partition("-")
        seeds.extend(range(int(first), int(last or first) + 1))
    return seeds


def _check(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_check(args.config, args.seed)


def _train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_batch(args.config, [args.seed], args.root, check_seed=args.check_seed)


def _batch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_batch(
        args.config, args.seeds, args.root, workers=args.workers, check_seed=args.check_seed
    )


def _watch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_watch(args.run_path)


def _chart(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_chart(
        args.root, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include, measure=args.measure
    )


def _evaluate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_evaluate(
        args.root, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include, workers=args.workers
    )


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rllab", description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=Path("runs"), help="the runs folder")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    check = sub.add_parser("check", help="run the method check of a config (PL-01)")
    check.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    check.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    check.set_defaults(run=_check)

    train = sub.add_parser("train", help="one training run: a one-seed batch (PL-03)")
    train.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)
    train.add_argument("--check-seed", type=int, default=0)
    train.set_defaults(run=_train)

    batch = sub.add_parser("batch", help="the method check, then one run per seed (PL-03)")
    batch.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    batch.add_argument("--seeds", type=parse_seeds, required=True, help="e.g. 1-5")
    batch.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
    batch.add_argument("--check-seed", type=int, default=0)
    batch.set_defaults(run=_batch)

    watch = sub.add_parser("watch", help="one snapshot of a run's progress (PL-04)")
    watch.add_argument("run_path", type=Path)
    watch.set_defaults(run=_watch)

    chart = sub.add_parser("chart", help="the learning curve of a world's runs (PL-05)")
    chart.add_argument("world")
    chart.add_argument("variant")
    chart.add_argument("--measure", default=None)
    chart.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[], metavar="NAME")
    chart.set_defaults(run=_chart)

    evaluate = sub.add_parser("evaluate", help="the final evaluation of a world's runs (PL-06)")
    evaluate.add_argument("world")
    evaluate.add_argument("variant")
    evaluate.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[], metavar="NAME")
    evaluate.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
    evaluate.set_defaults(run=_evaluate)
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Dispatch one subcommand to its pipeline entry and print what it returns."""
    args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = args.run(args)
    except Exception as exc:  # every error ends as one line naming its cause
        print(f"rllab {args.command}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    for item in result if isinstance(result, list) else [result]:
        print(item)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

U01.S91Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/contracts.py (381 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/contracts.py
"""Contract tools: the frozen types' JSON form, their schemas, and the doubles and fakes.

Each `tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/` folder holds what M1-T01 froze for that symbol:
- a type: `schema.json` (derived from the type by `schema_for`) and `samples/<case>.json`;
- a Protocol or class: `recording.json` and `double.py`, a replay double serving it;
- a function another component calls: `fake.py`, a fixture-backed fake with the real signature.

Tests are not on `sys.path`, so load this module by path:

    from pathlib import Path
    import runpy

    C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parents[2] / "contracts" / "contracts.py"))
    spec = C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "snake")          # a decoded sample
    world = C["double"]("core.World")                      # a replay double
    run_train = C["fake"]("train.run_train")               # a fake

JSON form: dataclasses are objects, `ActionSpace` members carry `"type": "Discrete" | "Box"`,
arrays are `{"dtype", "shape", "data"}`, paths and datetimes are strings, timedeltas seconds.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import runpy
import types
import typing
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

from rllab.baselines import BaselineMeans
from rllab.core import (
    EVAL_SEEDS,
    FINAL_SEEDS,
    ActionSpace,
    DrawState,
    Episode,
    EpisodeResult,
    Frame,
    Output,
    Step,
    TeamSpec,
    WorldSpec,
)
from rllab.evaluate import Comparison, Summary
from rllab.methods import MethodConfig, Transition
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Progress, RunRecord
from rllab.train import CheckResult, RunConfig

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

# Every type folder: symbol -> the type its samples decode to.
TYPES: dict[str, Any] = {
    "core.ActionSpace": ActionSpace,
    "core.TeamSpec": TeamSpec,
    "core.WorldSpec": WorldSpec,
    "core.Step": Step,
    "core.EpisodeResult": EpisodeResult,
    "core.DrawState": DrawState,
    "core.Frame": Frame,
    "core.Episode": Episode,
    "core.EVAL_SEEDS": tuple[int, ...],
    "core.FINAL_SEEDS": tuple[int, ...],
    "core.Output": Output,
    "baselines.BaselineMeans": BaselineMeans,
    "methods.MethodConfig": MethodConfig,
    "methods.Transition": Transition,
    "train.RunConfig": RunConfig,
    "train.CheckResult": CheckResult,
    "runlog.RunRecord": RunRecord,
    "runlog.EvalPoint": EvalPoint,
    "runlog.Checkpoint": Checkpoint,
    "runlog.Progress": Progress,
    "evaluate.Comparison": Comparison,
    "evaluate.Summary": Summary,
}
CONSTANTS: dict[str, object] = {"core.EVAL_SEEDS": EVAL_SEEDS, "core.FINAL_SEEDS": FINAL_SEEDS}

_UNIONS = (typing.Union, types.UnionType)
_SCALARS: dict[type, str] = {bool: "boolean", int: "integer", float: "number", str: "string"}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- the type walk
def _hints(cls: type) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return get_type_hints(cls)


def _is_array(tp: Any) -> bool:
    return tp is np.ndarray or get_origin(tp) is np.ndarray


def _is_mapping(tp: Any) -> bool:
    return get_origin(tp) in {Mapping, dict}


def _tagged(tp: Any) -> bool:
    """A union of dataclasses, told apart by a `"type"` key."""
    args = [a for a in get_args(tp) if a is not type(None)]
    return len(args) > 1 and all(dataclasses.is_dataclass(a) for a in args)


def schema_for(tp: Any, tag: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The JSON Schema of `tp`'s JSON form."""
    if tp is Any or tp is object:
        return {}
    if tp is type(None):
        return {"type": "null"}
    if tp in _SCALARS:
        return {"type": _SCALARS[tp]}
    if tp is Path:
        return {"type": "string"}
    if tp is datetime:
        return {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"}
    if tp is timedelta:
        return {"type": "number", "description": "seconds"}
    if _is_array(tp):
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "dtype": {"type": "string"},
                "shape": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "integer"}},
                "data": {"type": "array"},
            },
            "required": ["data", "dtype", "shape"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        return _dataclass_schema(tp, tag)
    return _generic_schema(tp)


def _dataclass_schema(tp: Any, tag: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    hints = _hints(tp)
    fields = dataclasses.fields(tp)
    props = {f.name: schema_for(hints[f.name]) for f in fields}
    required = [
        f.name
        for f in fields
        if f.default is dataclasses.MISSING and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING
    ]
    if tag:
        props = {"type": {"const": tag}, **props}
        required = ["type", *required]
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": props,
        "required": required,
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }


def _generic_schema(tp: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if origin is Literal:
        return {"enum": list(args)}
    if origin in _UNIONS:
        tagged = _tagged(tp)
        return {"anyOf": [schema_for(a, a.__name__ if tagged else None) for a in args]}
    if _is_mapping(tp):
        value = args[1] if args else Any
        return {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": schema_for(value)}
    if origin in {tuple, list} and (len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis or origin is list):
        return {"type": "array", "items": schema_for(args[0])}
    if origin is tuple:
        n = len(args)
        return {
            "type": "array",
            "prefixItems": [schema_for(a) for a in args],
            "minItems": n,
            "maxItems": n,
        }
    raise TypeError(f"no JSON form for {tp!r}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ decoding
def decode(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    """`data`, in JSON form, as a value of type `tp`; `ValueError` if it doesn't fit."""
    if tp is Any or tp is object:
        return data
    if tp is type(None):
        return _expect(data is None, data, tp)
    if tp is float:
        _expect(isinstance(data, (int, float)) and not isinstance(data, bool), data, tp)
        return float(data)
    if tp in _SCALARS:
        return _expect(type(data) is tp, data, tp)
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(_expect(isinstance(data, str), data, tp))
    if tp is datetime:
        return datetime.fromisoformat(_expect(isinstance(data, str), data, tp))
    if tp is timedelta:
        return timedelta(seconds=decode(float, data))
    if _is_array(tp):
        return np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=data["dtype"]).reshape(data["shape"])
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        _expect(isinstance(data, dict), data, tp)
        hints = _hints(tp)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        body = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k != "type"}
        return tp(**{k: decode(hints[k], v) for k, v in body.items()})  # type: ignore[operator]
    return _decode_generic(tp, data)


def _decode_generic(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if origin is Literal:
        return _expect(data in args, data, tp)
    if origin in _UNIONS:
        return _decode_union(tp, data)
    if _is_mapping(tp):
        _expect(isinstance(data, dict), data, tp)
        value = args[1] if args else Any
        return {k: decode(value, v) for k, v in data.items()}
    _expect(isinstance(data, list), data, tp)
    if origin is list:
        return [decode(args[0], v) for v in data]
    if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
        return tuple(decode(args[0], v) for v in data)
    _expect(len(args) == len(data), data, tp)
    return tuple(decode(a, v) for a, v in zip(args, data, strict=True))


def _decode_union(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    args = get_args(tp)
    if _tagged(tp) and isinstance(data, dict):
        match = [a for a in args if getattr(a, "__name__", None) == data.get("type")]
        _expect(len(match) == 1, data, tp)
        return decode(match[0], data)
    for arg in args:
        try:
            return decode(arg, data)
        except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
            continue
    raise ValueError(f"{data!r} fits none of {tp!r}")


def _expect(ok: bool, data: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
    if not ok:
        raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not a {tp!r}")
    return data


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ encoding
def encode(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    """`value`, of type `tp`, in JSON form (the inverse of `decode`)."""
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if value is None or tp in _SCALARS or tp is Any or tp is object or origin is Literal:
        return float(value) if tp is float else value
    if tp is Path:
        return str(value)
    if tp is datetime:
        return value.isoformat()
    if tp is timedelta:
        return value.total_seconds()
    if _is_array(tp):
        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        hints = _hints(tp)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        return {f.name: encode(hints[f.name], getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    if origin in _UNIONS:
        arg = next(a for a in args if _fits(a, value))
        out = encode(arg, value)
        return {"type": arg.__name__, **out} if _tagged(tp) else out
    if _is_mapping(tp):
        return {k: encode(args[1] if args else Any, v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if origin is list or (len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis):
        return [encode(args[0], v) for v in value]
    return [encode(a, v) for a, v in zip(args, value, strict=True)]


def _fits(tp: Any, value: Any) -> bool:
    if tp is type(None):
        return value is None
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp) or tp in {Path, datetime, timedelta}:
        return isinstance(value, tp)
    if tp is float:
        return isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool)
    if tp in _SCALARS:
        return type(value) is tp
    if _is_array(tp):
        return isinstance(value, np.ndarray)
    return True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- validation
def validate(instance: Any, schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str = "$") -> list[str]:
    """Each way `instance` breaks `schema` (the subset `schema_for` writes); empty if valid."""
    if "anyOf" in schema:
        if any(not validate(instance, s, path) for s in schema["anyOf"]):
            return []
        return [f"{path}: matches none of anyOf"]
    if "const" in schema and instance != schema["const"]:
        return [f"{path}: {instance!r} != {schema['const']!r}"]
    if "enum" in schema and instance not in schema["enum"]:
        return [f"{path}: {instance!r} not in {schema['enum']!r}"]
    kind = schema.get("type")
    if kind and not _is_kind(instance, kind):
        return [f"{path}: {instance!r} is not {kind}"]
    if kind == "object":
        return _validate_object(instance, schema, path)
    if kind == "array":
        return _validate_array(instance, schema, path)
    return []


def _is_kind(instance: Any, kind: str) -> bool:
    checks: dict[str, Callable[[Any], bool]] = {
        "object": lambda v: isinstance(v, dict),
        "array": lambda v: isinstance(v, list),
        "string": lambda v: isinstance(v, str),
        "integer": lambda v: isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool),
        "number": lambda v: isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool),
        "boolean": lambda v: isinstance(v, bool),
        "null": lambda v: v is None,
    }
    return checks[kind](instance)


def _validate_object(instance: dict[str, Any], schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = [f"{path}: missing {k!r}" for k in schema.get("required", []) if k not in instance]
    props = schema.get("properties", {})
    extra = schema.get("additionalProperties", True)
    for key, value in instance.items():
        if key in props:
            errors += validate(value, props[key], f"{path}.{key}")
        elif extra is False:
            errors.append(f"{path}: unexpected {key!r}")
        elif isinstance(extra, dict):
            errors += validate(value, extra, f"{path}.{key}")
    return errors


def _validate_array(instance: list[Any], schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    if len(instance) < schema.get("minItems", 0):
        errors.append(f"{path}: fewer than {schema['minItems']} items")
    if len(instance) > schema.get("maxItems", len(instance)):
        errors.append(f"{path}: more than {schema['maxItems']} items")
    for i, item in enumerate(instance):
        prefix = schema.get("prefixItems", [])
        sub = prefix[i] if i < len(prefix) else schema.get("items", {})
        errors += validate(item, sub, f"{path}[{i}]")
    return errors


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- access
def read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def raw(symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
    """A sample in JSON form."""
    return read_json(HERE / symbol / "samples" / f"{case}.json")


def cases(symbol: str) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(p.stem for p in (HERE / symbol / "samples").glob("*.json"))


def sample(symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
    """A sample as a value of the symbol's type."""
    return decode(TYPES[symbol], raw(symbol, case))


def recording(symbol: str) -> Any:
    """A replay double's recording, in JSON form."""
    return read_json(HERE / symbol / "recording.json")


def double(symbol: str, *args: Any) -> Any:
    """The replay double of a Protocol or class symbol."""
    return runpy.run_path(str(HERE / symbol / "double.py"))["double"](*args)


def fake(symbol: str) -> Any:
    """The fixture-backed fake of a function symbol, with the real signature."""
    return runpy.run_path(str(HERE / symbol / "fake.py"))[symbol.split(".")[-1]]
