"""Contract tools: the frozen types' JSON form, their schemas, and the doubles and fakes.

Each `tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/` folder holds what M1-T01 froze for that symbol:
- a type: `schema.json` (derived from the type by `schema_for`) and `samples/<case>.json`;
- a Protocol or class: `recording.json` and `double.py`, a replay double serving it;
- a function another component calls: `fake.py`, a fixture-backed fake with the real signature.

Tests are not on `sys.path`, so load this module by path:

    from pathlib import Path
    import runpy

    C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parents[2] / "contracts" / "contracts.py"))
    spec = C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "snake")          # a decoded sample
    world = C["double"]("core.World")                      # a replay double
    run_train = C["fake"]("train.run_train")               # a fake

JSON form: dataclasses are objects, `ActionSpace` members carry `"type": "Discrete" | "Box"`,
arrays are `{"dtype", "shape", "data"}`, paths and datetimes are strings, timedeltas seconds.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
import runpy
import types
import typing
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

from rllab.baselines import BaselineMeans
from rllab.core import (
    EVAL_SEEDS,
    FINAL_SEEDS,
    ActionSpace,
    DrawState,
    Episode,
    EpisodeResult,
    Frame,
    Output,
    Step,
    TeamSpec,
    WorldSpec,
)
from rllab.evaluate import Comparison, Summary
from rllab.methods import MethodConfig, Transition
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Progress, RunRecord
from rllab.train import CheckResult, RunConfig

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

# Every type folder: symbol -> the type its samples decode to.
TYPES: dict[str, Any] = {
    "core.ActionSpace": ActionSpace,
    "core.TeamSpec": TeamSpec,
    "core.WorldSpec": WorldSpec,
    "core.Step": Step,
    "core.EpisodeResult": EpisodeResult,
    "core.DrawState": DrawState,
    "core.Frame": Frame,
    "core.Episode": Episode,
    "core.EVAL_SEEDS": tuple[int, ...],
    "core.FINAL_SEEDS": tuple[int, ...],
    "core.Output": Output,
    "baselines.BaselineMeans": BaselineMeans,
    "methods.MethodConfig": MethodConfig,
    "methods.Transition": Transition,
    "train.RunConfig": RunConfig,
    "train.CheckResult": CheckResult,
    "runlog.RunRecord": RunRecord,
    "runlog.EvalPoint": EvalPoint,
    "runlog.Checkpoint": Checkpoint,
    "runlog.Progress": Progress,
    "evaluate.Comparison": Comparison,
    "evaluate.Summary": Summary,
}
CONSTANTS: dict[str, object] = {"core.EVAL_SEEDS": EVAL_SEEDS, "core.FINAL_SEEDS": FINAL_SEEDS}

NONE = type(None)
_UNIONS = (typing.Union, types.UnionType)
_SCALARS: dict[Any, str] = {bool: "boolean", int: "integer", float: "number", str: "string"}
_ARRAY_SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {
        "dtype": {"type": "string"},
        "shape": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "integer"}},
        "data": {"type": "array"},
    },
    "required": ["data", "dtype", "shape"],
    "additionalProperties": False,
}
_LEAF_SCHEMAS: dict[Any, dict[str, Any]] = {
    NONE: {"type": "null"},
    **{tp: {"type": name} for tp, name in _SCALARS.items()},
    Path: {"type": "string"},
    datetime: {"type": "string", "format": "date-time"},
    timedelta: {"type": "number", "description": "seconds"},
}


def _expect(ok: bool, data: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
    if not ok:
        raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not a {tp!r}")
    return data


def _is_number(v: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool)


_LEAF_DECODERS: dict[Any, Callable[[Any], Any]] = {
    NONE: lambda d: _expect(d is None, d, None),
    bool: lambda d: _expect(type(d) is bool, d, bool),
    int: lambda d: _expect(type(d) is int, d, int),
    str: lambda d: _expect(type(d) is str, d, str),
    float: lambda d: float(_expect(_is_number(d), d, float)),
    Path: lambda d: Path(_expect(isinstance(d, str), d, Path)),
    datetime: lambda d: datetime.fromisoformat(_expect(isinstance(d, str), d, datetime)),
    timedelta: lambda d: timedelta(seconds=_expect(_is_number(d), d, timedelta)),
}
_LEAF_ENCODERS: dict[Any, Callable[[Any], Any]] = {
    bool: lambda v: v,
    int: lambda v: v,
    str: lambda v: v,
    float: float,
    Path: str,
    datetime: lambda v: v.isoformat(),
    timedelta: lambda v: v.total_seconds(),
}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- the type walk
def _is_array(tp: Any) -> bool:
    return tp is np.ndarray or get_origin(tp) is np.ndarray


def _is_mapping(tp: Any) -> bool:
    return get_origin(tp) in {Mapping, dict}


def _is_seq(tp: Any) -> bool:
    """`list[X]` or `tuple[X, ...]`."""
    args = get_args(tp)
    return get_origin(tp) is list or (len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis)


def _tagged(tp: Any) -> bool:
    """A union of dataclasses, told apart by a `"type"` key."""
    args = [a for a in get_args(tp) if a is not NONE]
    return len(args) > 1 and all(dataclasses.is_dataclass(a) for a in args)


def _free(tp: Any) -> bool:
    return tp is Any or tp is object


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- schemas
def schema_for(tp: Any, tag: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The JSON Schema of `tp`'s JSON form."""
    if _free(tp):
        return {}
    if tp in _LEAF_SCHEMAS:
        return dict(_LEAF_SCHEMAS[tp])
    if _is_array(tp):
        return json.loads(json.dumps(_ARRAY_SCHEMA))
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        return _dataclass_schema(tp, tag)
    return _generic_schema(tp)


def _dataclass_schema(tp: Any, tag: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    hints = get_type_hints(tp)
    fields = dataclasses.fields(tp)
    props = {f.name: schema_for(hints[f.name]) for f in fields}
    required = [
        f.name
        for f in fields
        if f.default is dataclasses.MISSING and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING
    ]
    if tag:
        props = {"type": {"const": tag}, **props}
        required = ["type", *required]
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": props,
        "required": required,
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }


def _generic_schema(tp: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if origin is Literal:
        return {"enum": list(args)}
    if origin in _UNIONS:
        tagged = _tagged(tp)
        return {"anyOf": [schema_for(a, a.__name__ if tagged else None) for a in args]}
    if _is_mapping(tp):
        return {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": schema_for(args[1] if args else Any)}
    if _is_seq(tp):
        return {"type": "array", "items": schema_for(args[0])}
    if origin is tuple:
        n = len(args)
        items = [schema_for(a) for a in args]
        return {"type": "array", "prefixItems": items, "minItems": n, "maxItems": n}
    raise TypeError(f"no JSON form for {tp!r}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ decoding
def decode(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    """`data`, in JSON form, as a value of type `tp`; `ValueError` if it doesn't fit."""
    if _free(tp):
        return data
    if tp in _LEAF_DECODERS:
        return _LEAF_DECODERS[tp](data)
    if _is_array(tp):
        return np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=data["dtype"]).reshape(data["shape"])
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        _expect(isinstance(data, dict), data, tp)
        hints = get_type_hints(tp)
        body = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k != "type"}
        return tp(**{k: decode(hints[k], v) for k, v in body.items()})
    return _decode_generic(tp, data)


def _decode_generic(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if origin is Literal:
        return _expect(data in args, data, tp)
    if origin in _UNIONS:
        return _decode_union(tp, data)
    if _is_mapping(tp):
        _expect(isinstance(data, dict), data, tp)
        return {k: decode(args[1] if args else Any, v) for k, v in data.items()}
    _expect(isinstance(data, list), data, tp)
    if _is_seq(tp):
        items = [decode(args[0], v) for v in data]
        return items if origin is list else tuple(items)
    _expect(len(args) == len(data), data, tp)
    return tuple(decode(a, v) for a, v in zip(args, data, strict=True))


def _decode_union(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    args = get_args(tp)
    if _tagged(tp) and isinstance(data, dict):
        match = [a for a in args if getattr(a, "__name__", None) == data.get("type")]
        _expect(len(match) == 1, data, tp)
        return decode(match[0], data)
    for arg in args:
        try:
            return decode(arg, data)
        except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
            continue
    raise ValueError(f"{data!r} fits none of {tp!r}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ encoding
def encode(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    """`value`, of type `tp`, in JSON form (the inverse of `decode`)."""
    if value is None or _free(tp) or get_origin(tp) is Literal:
        return value
    if tp in _LEAF_ENCODERS:
        return _LEAF_ENCODERS[tp](value)
    if _is_array(tp):
        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        hints = get_type_hints(tp)
        return {f.name: encode(hints[f.name], getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
    return _encode_generic(tp, value)


def _encode_generic(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if origin in _UNIONS:
        arg = next(a for a in args if _fits(a, value))
        out = encode(arg, value)
        return {"type": arg.__name__, **out} if _tagged(tp) else out
    if _is_mapping(tp):
        return {k: encode(args[1] if args else Any, v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if _is_seq(tp):
        return [encode(args[0], v) for v in value]
    return [encode(a, v) for a, v in zip(args, value, strict=True)]


def _fits(tp: Any, value: Any) -> bool:
    if tp is NONE:
        return value is None
    if tp is float:
        return _is_number(value)
    if tp in _SCALARS:
        return type(value) is tp
    if _is_array(tp):
        return isinstance(value, np.ndarray)
    return isinstance(value, tp) if isinstance(tp, type) else True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- validation
def validate(instance: Any, schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str = "$") -> list[str]:
    """Each way `instance` breaks `schema` (the subset `schema_for` writes); empty if valid."""
    if "anyOf" in schema:
        ok = any(not validate(instance, s, path) for s in schema["anyOf"])
        return [] if ok else [f"{path}: {instance!r} matches none of anyOf"]
    errors = _keyword_errors(instance, schema, path)
    kind = schema.get("type")
    if errors or kind not in {"object", "array"}:
        return errors
    if kind == "object":
        return _validate_object(instance, schema, path)
    return _validate_array(instance, schema, path)


_KINDS: dict[str, Callable[[Any], bool]] = {
    "object": lambda v: isinstance(v, dict),
    "array": lambda v: isinstance(v, list),
    "string": lambda v: isinstance(v, str),
    "integer": lambda v: isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool),
    "number": _is_number,
    "boolean": lambda v: isinstance(v, bool),
    "null": lambda v: v is None,
}


def _keyword_errors(instance: Any, schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    if "const" in schema and instance != schema["const"]:
        errors.append(f"{path}: {instance!r} != {schema['const']!r}")
    if "enum" in schema and instance not in schema["enum"]:
        errors.append(f"{path}: {instance!r} not in {schema['enum']!r}")
    if "type" in schema and not _KINDS[schema["type"]](instance):
        errors.append(f"{path}: {instance!r} is not {schema['type']}")
    return errors


def _validate_object(instance: dict[str, Any], schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = [f"{path}: missing {k!r}" for k in schema.get("required", []) if k not in instance]
    props = schema.get("properties", {})
    extra = schema.get("additionalProperties", True)
    for key, value in instance.items():
        if key in props:
            errors += validate(value, props[key], f"{path}.{key}")
        elif extra is False:
            errors.append(f"{path}: unexpected {key!r}")
        elif isinstance(extra, dict):
            errors += validate(value, extra, f"{path}.{key}")
    return errors


def _validate_array(instance: list[Any], schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    if len(instance) < schema.get("minItems", 0):
        errors.append(f"{path}: fewer than {schema['minItems']} items")
    if len(instance) > schema.get("maxItems", len(instance)):
        errors.append(f"{path}: more than {schema['maxItems']} items")
    prefix = schema.get("prefixItems", [])
    for i, item in enumerate(instance):
        sub = prefix[i] if i < len(prefix) else schema.get("items", {})
        errors += validate(item, sub, f"{path}[{i}]")
    return errors


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- access
def read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def raw(symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
    """A sample in JSON form."""
    return read_json(HERE / symbol / "samples" / f"{case}.json")


def cases(symbol: str) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(p.stem for p in (HERE / symbol / "samples").glob("*.json"))


def sample(symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
    """A sample as a value of the symbol's type."""
    return decode(TYPES[symbol], raw(symbol, case))


def recording(symbol: str) -> Any:
    """A replay double's recording, in JSON form."""
    return read_json(HERE / symbol / "recording.json")


def double(symbol: str, *args: Any) -> Any:
    """The replay double of a Protocol or class symbol."""
    return runpy.run_path(str(HERE / symbol / "double.py"))["double"](*args)


def fake(symbol: str) -> Any:
    """The fixture-backed fake of a function symbol, with the real signature."""
    return runpy.run_path(str(HERE / symbol / "fake.py"))[symbol.split(".")[-1]]

\"array\": lambda v: isinstance(v, list),\n    \"string\": lambda v: isinstance(v, str),\n    \"integer\": lambda v: isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool),\n    \"number\": _is_number,\n    \"boolean\": lambda v: isinstance(v, bool),\n    \"null\": lambda v: v is None,\n}\n\n\ndef _keyword_errors(instance: Any, schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> list[str]:\n    errors = []\n    if schema.get(\"required\", []) if k not in instance]\n    props = schema.get(\"properties\", {})\n    extra = schema.get(\"additionalProperties\", True)\n    for key, value in instance.items():\n        if key in props:\n            errors += validate(value, props[key], f\"{path}.{key}\")\n        elif extra is False:\n            errors.append(f\"{path}: unexpected {key!r}\")\n        elif isinstance(extra, dict):\n            errors += validate(value, extra, f\"{path}.{key}\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef _validate_array(instance: list[Any], schema: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> list[str]:\n    errors = []\n    if len(instance) < schema.get(\"minItems\", 0):\n        errors.append(f\"{path}: fewer than {schema['minItems']} items\")\n    if len(instance) > def dump(path: Path, data) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def put(symbol: str, case: str, value) -> None:
    dump(CT / symbol / "samples" / f"{case}.json", enc(C["TYPES"][symbol], value))


def f32(rows):
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.float32)


UTC = timezone.utc

    "fish", "local", tuple(f"fish-{i}" for i in range(8)), (FISH_TEAM,), 24,

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ snake episode, seed 42
def snake_obs(danger, heading, food, length):
    h = [0.0] * 4
    h[["up", "right", "down", "left"].index(heading)] = 1.0
    fd = [1.0 if d in food else 0.0 for d in ["up", "right", "down", "left"]]
    return [*map(float, danger), *h, *fd, float(length)]


def snake_draw(body, food):
    objs = [Drawable("head", *body[0], 1.0, "snake", 0)]
    objs += [Drawable("body", x, y, 1.0, "snake", 0) for x, y in body[1:]]
    objs.append(Drawable("food", *food, 1.0, None, None))
    return DrawState(10.0, 10.0, tuple(objs))

            "obs": enc(np.ndarray, f32([learner_obs[i]])),
            "action": enc(np.ndarray, np.array([[0, 1, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0][i]])),
            "transition": enc(Transition, Transition(
                f32([learner_obs[i]]), np.array([[0, 1, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0][i]]),
                f32([rewards42[i % 5 + 1]]), f32([learner_next[i]]), i == 9, i == 9, np.array([True]),
            )),
            "state": {"updates": i + 1, "epsilon": round(1.0 - 0.000095 * (i + 1), 6)},
        }
        for i in range(10)
    ],
    "policy_calls": policy_calls,
})

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ train
def cfg(seed, name="snake-default"):
    return RunConfig(f"{name}-s{seed}", "snake", "default", {"snake": DQN}, seed, 2000, {"dqn": {"epsilon_decay_steps": 500}})


put("train.RunConfig", "snake", cfg(1))
PREY_PPO = MethodConfig("ppo", dict(PPO.settings, lr=0.0003))
put("train.RunConfig", "prey_two_methods", RunConfig(
    "prey-default-s1", "prey", "default", {"prey": PREY_PPO, "predator": PREY_PPO}, 1, 200_000,
    {"ppo": {"rollout_steps": 256}},
))
dump(CT / "train.load_run_configs" / "samples" / "snake_s1_s2.json", [enc(RunConfig, cfg(1)), enc(RunConfig, cfg(2))])
put("train.CheckResult", "dqn_pass", CheckResult("dqn", "check-discrete", 1.0, True))
put("train.CheckResult", "ppo_fail", CheckResult("ppo", "check-continuous", 0.8, False))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ runlog
T0 = datetime(2026, 10, 18, 9, 0, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
def record(seed, status, failure=None, ended=True):
    c = cfg(seed)
    return RunRecord(c, SNAKE, T0, T0 + timedelta(minutes=41) if ended else None, status, failure, Path(f"runs/snake/default/{c.name}"))

    for p in (0, 50, 100)

        + [{"call": "episode", "result": enc(EpisodeResult, e)} for e in eps[6:]]
        + [{"call": "eval", "point": enc(EvalPoint, e)} for e in evals[11:]]

def dump(path, data):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def put(symbol, case, value):
    dump(CT / symbol / "samples" / f"{case}.json", enc(C["TYPES"][symbol], value))


def f32(rows):
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.float32)


def i64(rows):
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)


def arr(a):
    return enc(np.ndarray, a)


def yes(n=1):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=bool)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ specs

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ snake episode, seed 42
def snake_obs(danger, heading, food, length):
    dirs = ["up", "right", "down", "left"]
    return [
        *map(float, danger),
        *(1.0 if d == heading else 0.0 for d in dirs),
        *(1.0 if d in food else 0.0 for d in dirs),
        float(length),
    ]


def snake_draw(body, food):
    objs = [Drawable("head", *map(float, body[0]), 1.0, "snake", 0)]
    objs += [Drawable("body", float(x), float(y), 1.0, "snake", 0) for x, y in body[1:]]
    objs.append(Drawable("food", *map(float, food), 1.0, None, None))
    return DrawState(10.0, 10.0, tuple(objs))


# head first; x right, y down; starts heading right; actions 0 straight, 1 left, 2 right

    [(5, 5), (4, 5), (3, 5)],
    [(6, 5), (5, 5), (4, 5)],
    [(7, 5), (6, 5), (5, 5), (4, 5)],

]
foods = [(7, 5), (7, 5), (2, 8), (2, 8), (2, 8), (2, 8)]

    return enc(Step, Step(f32([obs]), f32([reward]), yes(), done, False))

                enc(
                    DrawState,
                    DrawState(
                        2.0,
                        1.0,
                        (
                            Drawable("target", 1.0, 0.5, 0.1, None, None),
                            Drawable("agent", x, 0.5, 0.1, "agent", 0),
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                for x in (0.5, 0.7, 0.9)
            ],
            "result": enc(
                EpisodeResult, EpisodeResult(3, 2, {"agent": -0.4}, {"agent": -0.4}, {})
            ),
        },
    },
)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ baselines
put(
    "baselines.BaselineMeans",
    "snake",
    BaselineMeans("snake", "default", {"snake": 0.35}, {"snake": 9.4}),
)
put(
    "baselines.BaselineMeans",
    "prey",
    BaselineMeans("prey", "default", {"prey": 0.2, "predator": 0.4}, {"prey": 1.1, "predator": 2.6}),
)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ methods
DQN = MethodConfig("dqn", dict(DEFAULTS))
PPO = MethodConfig(
    "ppo",
    {
        "lr": 0.001,
        "gamma": 0.99,
        "hidden": 64,
        "rollout_steps": 2048,
        "epochs": 10,
        "clip": 0.2,
        "gae_lambda": 0.95,
    },
)
put("methods.MethodConfig", "snake_dqn_defaults", DQN)
put("methods.MethodConfig", "ppo_lr_override", PPO)
put(
    "methods.Transition",
    "snake",
    Transition(f32([obs42[1]]), i64([0]), f32([1.0]), f32([obs42[2]]), False, False, yes()),
)
rngs = [np.random.default_rng(s) for s in (8, 9, 10)]
put(
    "methods.Transition",
    "many_fish",
    Transition(
        rngs[0].uniform(-1, 1, (8, 24)).round(3).astype(np.float32),
        rngs[2].uniform(-1, 1, (8, 2)).round(3).astype(np.float32),
        f32([0, 0, -1, 0, 0, 0, 0, -1]),
        rngs[1].uniform(-1, 1, (8, 24)).round(3).astype(np.float32),
        False,
        False,
        yes(8),
    ),
)
learner_actions = [0, 1, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0, 2, 0, 0]
dump(
    CT / "methods.Learner" / "recording.json",
    {
        "spec": enc(WorldSpec, SNAKE),
        "config": enc(MethodConfig, DQN),
        "team": "snake",
        "rng_seed": 0,
        "steps": [
            {
                "obs": arr(f32([obs42[i % 5]])),
                "action": arr(i64([a])),
                "transition": enc(
                    Transition,
                    Transition(
                        f32([obs42[i % 5]]),
                        i64([a]),
                        f32([rewards42[i % 5 + 1]]),
                        f32([obs42[i % 5 + 1]]),
                        i == 9,
                        i == 9,
                        yes(),
                    ),
                ),
                "state": {"updates": i + 1, "epsilon": round(1.0 - 0.000095 * (i + 1), 6)},
            }
            for i, a in enumerate(learner_actions)
        ],
        "policy_calls": policy_calls,
    },
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ train
def cfg(seed):
    return RunConfig(
        f"snake-default-s{seed}",
        "snake",
        "default",
        {"snake": DQN},
        seed,
        2000,
        {"dqn": {"epsilon_decay_steps": 500}},
    )


put("train.RunConfig", "snake", cfg(1))
PREY_PPO = MethodConfig("ppo", {**PPO.settings, "lr": 0.0003})
put(
    "train.RunConfig",
    "prey_two_methods",
    RunConfig(
        "prey-default-s1",
        "prey",
        "default",
        {"prey": PREY_PPO, "predator": PPO},
        1,
        200_000,
        {"ppo": {"rollout_steps": 256}},
    ),
)
dump(
    CT / "train.load_run_configs" / "samples" / "snake_s1_s2.json",
    [enc(RunConfig, cfg(1)), enc(RunConfig, cfg(2))],
)
put("train.CheckResult", "dqn_pass", CheckResult("dqn", "check-discrete", 1.0, True))
put("train.CheckResult", "ppo_fail", CheckResult("ppo", "check-continuous", 0.8, False))
dump(CT / "train.run_check" / "samples" / "dqn_pass.json", [enc(CheckResult, CheckResult("dqn", "check-discrete", 1.0, True))])

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ runlog
T0 = datetime(2026, 10, 18, 9, 0, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def record(seed, status, ended=True):
    c = cfg(seed)
    end = T0 + timedelta(minutes=41) if ended else None
    return RunRecord(c, SNAKE, T0, end, status, None, Path(f"runs/snake/default/{c.name}"))


put("runlog.RunRecord", "finished", record(1, "finished"))
s4 = RunConfig(
    "snake-default-s4",
    "snake",
    "default",
    {"snake": DQN},
    4,
    100_000,
    {"dqn": {"epsilon_decay_steps": 500}},
)

        Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s4"),
    ),
)
put("runlog.RunRecord", "incomplete", record(5, "incomplete", ended=False))

put(
    "runlog.EvalPoint",
    "prey_both_teams",
    EvalPoint(100_000, {"prey": 1.35, "predator": 3.1}, {"prey surviving": 2.4}),
)
RUN = Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1")


def ckpt(pct, state):
    return Checkpoint(
        "snake-default-s1", pct, 20 * pct, SNAKE, {"snake": DQN}, {"snake": state},
        RUN / "checkpoints" / f"ckpt_{pct}.pt",
    )


put("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_0pct", ckpt(0, {"updates": 0, "epsilon": 1.0}))
put("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_100pct", ckpt(100, {"updates": 1950, "epsilon": 0.05}))
put(
    "runlog.Progress",
    "snake_35pct",
    Progress(
        700, 2000, EvalPoint(700, {"snake": 2.1}, {}), timedelta(minutes=14, seconds=30), "running"
    ),
)
dump(
    CT / "runlog.progress" / "samples" / "snake_35pct.json",
    C["read_json"](CT / "runlog.Progress" / "samples" / "snake_35pct.json"),
)

# the finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder (budget 2000; checkpoints at 0, 50 and 100%)
run_rec = record(1, "finished")
evals = [EvalPoint(s, {"snake": round(s / 2000 * 8.5, 3)}, {}) for s in range(0, 2001, 100)]
eps = [
    EpisodeResult(1000 + i, 40 + 10 * i, {"snake": float(i % 4)}, {"snake": float(i % 4) - 1.0}, {})
    for i in range(12)
]
ckpts = [
    ckpt(p, {"updates": 19 * p, "epsilon": round(max(0.05, 1 - 0.019 * p), 3)}) for p in (0, 50, 100)
]
dump(
    CT / "runlog.Run" / "recording.json",
    {
        "record": enc(RunRecord, run_rec),
        "episodes": [enc(EpisodeResult, e) for e in eps],
        "evals": [enc(EvalPoint, e) for e in evals],
        "checkpoints": [enc(Checkpoint, c) for c in ckpts],
    },
)


def ck_call(c):
    return {"call": "checkpoint", "pct": c.pct, "step": c.step, "state": c.state, "returns": enc(Checkpoint, c)}


dump(
    CT / "runlog.RunWriter" / "recording.json",
    {
        "root": "runs",
        "config": enc(RunConfig, run_rec.config),
        "spec": enc(WorldSpec, SNAKE),
        "calls": [
            ck_call(ckpts[0]),
            *({"call": "episode", "result": enc(EpisodeResult, e)} for e in eps[:6]),
            *({"call": "eval", "point": enc(EvalPoint, e)} for e in evals[:11]),
            ck_call(ckpts[1]),
            *({"call": "episode", "result": enc(EpisodeResult, e)} for e in eps[6:]),
            *({"call": "eval", "point": enc(EvalPoint, e)} for e in evals[11:]),
            ck_call(ckpts[2]),
            {"call": "close", "status": "finished", "failure": None, "returns": enc(RunRecord, run_rec)},
        ],
    },
)

U01.S109Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/core.World/double.py (59 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/core.World/double.py
"""Replay double of `core.World`: a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42.

Recorded from `make("snake", max_steps=5)` under the snake rule (`core.Policy`'s double), so the
fifth step hits the step limit. It serves only its recording: another seed, or actions other than
the recorded ones, raise `ValueError`; a step after `done` raises `RuntimeError`, as `World` says.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import DrawState, EpisodeResult, Step, WorldSpec

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


class ReplayWorld:
    def __init__(self, recording: dict) -> None:
        self.spec: WorldSpec = C["decode"](WorldSpec, recording["spec"])
        self._seed = recording["seed"]
        self._reset = C["decode"](Step, recording["reset"])
        self._steps = [
            (C["decode"](np.ndarray, s["actions"]), C["decode"](Step, s["step"]))
            for s in recording["steps"]
        ]
        self._draws = [C["decode"](DrawState, d) for d in recording["draws"]]
        self._result = C["decode"](EpisodeResult, recording["result"])
        self._t: int | None = None
        self.actions: list[np.ndarray] = []

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step:
        if seed != self._seed:
            raise ValueError(f"the replay world has seed {self._seed} only, not {seed}")
        self._t = 0
        self.actions = []
        return self._reset

    def step(self, actions: np.ndarray) -> Step:
        if self._t is None:
            raise RuntimeError("step before reset")
        if self._t >= len(self._steps):
            raise RuntimeError("step after done")
        expected, step = self._steps[self._t]
        if not np.array_equal(np.asarray(actions), expected):
            raise ValueError(f"step {self._t + 1}: recorded actions {expected}, got {actions}")
        self.actions.append(np.asarray(actions))
        self._t += 1
        return step

    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult:
        return self._result

    def draw(self) -> DrawState:
        return self._draws[self._t or 0]


def double() -> ReplayWorld:
    return ReplayWorld(C["recording"]("core.World"))

"""Replay double of `core.Policy`: the snake rule over 5 recorded observations.

The observations are those of `core.World`'s seed-42 recording, steps 0 to 4. `act` returns the
recorded action for a recorded observation and raises `ValueError` for any other.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


class ReplayPolicy:
    def __init__(self, calls: list[dict]) -> None:
        self._calls = [
            (C["decode"](np.ndarray, c["obs"]), C["decode"](np.ndarray, c["action"])) for c in calls
        ]
        self.seen: list[np.ndarray] = []

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
        self.seen.append(np.asarray(obs))
        for recorded, action in self._calls:
            if np.array_equal(recorded, obs):
                return action.copy()
        raise ValueError(f"observation not in the recording: {obs}")


def double() -> ReplayPolicy:
    return ReplayPolicy(C["recording"]("core.Policy")["calls"])

"""Replay double of `baselines.RandomPolicy`: 5 snake actions and 5 fish pushes from a fixed rng.

`double("snake")` (the default) or `double("fish")`. Each `act` returns the next recorded action;
the values stand for draws from `np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)`, not for the real draw order.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import ActionSpace

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


class ReplayRandomPolicy:
    def __init__(self, recording: dict) -> None:
        self.action: ActionSpace = C["decode"](ActionSpace, recording["action"])
        self.rng_seed: int = recording["rng_seed"]
        self.obs: np.ndarray = C["decode"](np.ndarray, recording["obs"])
        self._actions = [C["decode"](np.ndarray, a) for a in recording["actions"]]
        self._next = 0

        if self._next >= len(self._actions):
            raise ValueError(f"the recording has {len(self._actions)} actions only")
        self._next += 1
        return self._actions[self._next - 1].copy()


def double(case: str = "snake") -> ReplayRandomPolicy:
    return ReplayRandomPolicy(C["recording"]("baselines.RandomPolicy")[case])

U01.S114Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/methods.Learner/double.py (69 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/methods.Learner/double.py
"""Replay double of `methods.Learner`: a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions.

`act` returns the recorded exploring action for each recorded observation, in order; `observe`
takes the recorded transitions in order (anything else raises `ValueError`), and `state_dict`
is the recorded state after the last `observe`. `policy()` is the snake rule's replay double: a
frozen, deterministic policy, unchanged by later `observe` calls. Set `fail_at` to the number of
the `observe` call that raises `NonFiniteError(nan)` instead, before any state change.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.methods import MethodConfig, NonFiniteError, Transition

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
C = runpy.run_path(str(HERE.parent / "contracts.py"))
ReplayPolicy = runpy.run_path(str(HERE.parent / "core.Policy" / "double.py"))["ReplayPolicy"]


def same_transition(a: Transition, b: Transition) -> bool:
    arrays = ("obs", "actions", "rewards", "next_obs", "active")
    return all(np.array_equal(getattr(a, f), getattr(b, f)) for f in arrays) and (
        (a.done, a.truncated) == (b.done, b.truncated)
    )


class ReplayLearner:
    def __init__(self, recording: dict, fail_at: int | None = None) -> None:
        self.config: MethodConfig = C["decode"](MethodConfig, recording["config"])
        self.team: str = recording["team"]
        steps = recording["steps"]
        self._acts = [
            (C["decode"](np.ndarray, s["obs"]), C["decode"](np.ndarray, s["action"])) for s in steps
        ]
        self._transitions = [C["decode"](Transition, s["transition"]) for s in steps]
        self._states = [s["state"] for s in steps]
        self._policy_calls = recording["policy_calls"]
        self._acted = 0
        self._observed = 0
        self.fail_at = fail_at

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
        recorded, action = self._acts[self._acted % len(self._acts)]
        if not np.array_equal(recorded, obs):
            raise ValueError(f"act {self._acted + 1}: recorded obs {recorded}, got {obs}")
        self._acted += 1
        return action.copy()

    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        if self.fail_at is not None and self._observed + 1 >= self.fail_at:
            raise NonFiniteError(float("nan"))
        expected = self._transitions[self._observed % len(self._transitions)]
        if not same_transition(expected, t):
            raise ValueError(f"observe {self._observed + 1}: not the recorded transition")
        self._observed += 1

    def policy(self) -> ReplayPolicy:
        return ReplayPolicy(self._policy_calls)

    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        if self._observed == 0:
            return {"updates": 0, "epsilon": 1.0}
        return dict(self._states[(self._observed - 1) % len(self._states)])


def double(fail_at: int | None = None) -> ReplayLearner:
    return ReplayLearner(C["recording"]("methods.Learner"), fail_at)

  "content": "\"\"\"Replay double of `methods.Learner`: a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions.\n\n`act` returns the recorded exploring action for each recorded observation, in order; `observe`\ntakes the recorded transitions in order (anything else raises `ValueError`), and `state_dict`\nis the recorded state after the last `observe`. `policy()` is the snake rule's replay double: a\nfrozen, deterministic policy, unchanged by later `observe` calls. U01.S116Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/core.World/double.py (64 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/core.World/double.py
"""Replay double of `core.World`: a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42.

Recorded from `make("snake", max_steps=5)` under the snake rule (`core.Policy`'s double), so the
fifth step hits the step limit. Every `reset` starts the recording again, whatever the seed (kept
in `seeds`), and each `step` returns the next recorded step, whatever the actions (kept in
`actions`; compare them with `recorded_actions`). As `World` says, an action outside
`spec.action` raises `ActionError` and a step after `done` raises `RuntimeError`.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import ActionError, Discrete, DrawState, EpisodeResult, Step, WorldSpec

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


class ReplayWorld:
    def __init__(self, recording: dict) -> None:
        self.spec: WorldSpec = C["decode"](WorldSpec, recording["spec"])
        self.recorded_seed: int = recording["seed"]
        self.recorded_actions = [C["decode"](np.ndarray, s["actions"]) for s in recording["steps"]]
        self._reset = C["decode"](Step, recording["reset"])
        self._steps = [C["decode"](Step, s["step"]) for s in recording["steps"]]
        self._draws = [C["decode"](DrawState, d) for d in recording["draws"]]
        self._result = C["decode"](EpisodeResult, recording["result"])
        self._t: int | None = None
        self.seeds: list[int] = []
        self.actions: list[np.ndarray] = []

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step:
        self._t = 0
        self.seeds.append(seed)
        self.actions = []
        return self._reset

    def step(self, actions: np.ndarray) -> Step:
        if self._t is None or self._t >= len(self._steps):
            raise RuntimeError("step after done (or before reset)")
        actions = np.asarray(actions)
        for agent, value in zip(self.spec.agents, actions, strict=True):
            if not self._inside(value):
                raise ActionError(agent, value.tolist())
        self.actions.append(actions)
        self._t += 1
        return self._steps[self._t - 1]

    def _inside(self, value: np.ndarray) -> bool:
        space = self.spec.action
        if isinstance(space, Discrete):
            return value.shape == () and float(value).is_integer() and 0 <= value < space.n
        return value.shape == (space.dims,) and bool(np.all((value >= space.low) & (value <= space.high)))

    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult:
        return self._result

    def draw(self) -> DrawState:
        return self._draws[self._t or 0]


def double() -> ReplayWorld:
    return ReplayWorld(C["recording"]("core.World"))

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/methods.Learner/double.py
"""Replay double of `methods.Learner`: a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions.

The 10 transitions are two passes of `core.World`'s seed-42 recording. `act` returns the recorded
exploring actions in turn (cycling), `observe` keeps each transition in `observed` and moves to
the next recorded state, and `state_dict` is the recorded state after the last `observe`.
`policy()` is the snake rule's replay double: frozen and deterministic, unchanged by later
`observe` calls. `double(fail_at=n)` makes the n-th `observe` raise `NonFiniteError(nan)` before
any state change.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.methods import MethodConfig, NonFiniteError, Transition

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
C = runpy.run_path(str(HERE.parent / "contracts.py"))
ReplayPolicy = runpy.run_path(str(HERE.parent / "core.Policy" / "double.py"))["ReplayPolicy"]


class ReplayLearner:
    def __init__(self, recording: dict, fail_at: int | None = None) -> None:
        self.config: MethodConfig = C["decode"](MethodConfig, recording["config"])
        self.team: str = recording["team"]
        steps = recording["steps"]
        self.recorded_transitions = [C["decode"](Transition, s["transition"]) for s in steps]
        self._actions = [C["decode"](np.ndarray, s["action"]) for s in steps]
        self._states = [s["state"] for s in steps]
        self._policy_calls = recording["policy_calls"]
        self._acted = 0
        self.observed: list[Transition] = []
        self.fail_at = fail_at

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:
        self._acted += 1
        return self._actions[(self._acted - 1) % len(self._actions)].copy()

    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        if self.fail_at is not None and len(self.observed) + 1 == self.fail_at:
            raise NonFiniteError(float("nan"))
        self.observed.append(t)

    def policy(self) -> ReplayPolicy:
        return ReplayPolicy(self._policy_calls)

    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        if not self.observed:
            return {"updates": 0, "epsilon": 1.0}
        return dict(self._states[(len(self.observed) - 1) % len(self._states)])


def double(fail_at: int | None = None) -> ReplayLearner:
    return ReplayLearner(C["recording"]("methods.Learner"), fail_at)

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/runlog.Run/double.py
"""Replay double of `runlog.Run`: a finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder.

Budget 2,000 steps: 12 training episodes, 21 evaluation points (every 5%) and checkpoints at 0,
50 and 100%. `double()` is seed 1 as recorded; `double(seed, status)` is the same folder renamed
to `snake-default-s<seed>` with that status, for a batch of runs.
"""

import dataclasses
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, RunRecord

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


class ReplayRun:
    def __init__(self, recording: dict, seed: int, status: str) -> None:
        recorded: RunRecord = C["decode"](RunRecord, recording["record"])
        name = f"snake-default-s{seed}"
        path = recorded.path.parent / name
        config = dataclasses.replace(recorded.config, name=name, seed=seed)
        ended = recorded.ended if status in {"finished", "failed"} else None
        self.record = dataclasses.replace(
            recorded, config=config, status=status, ended=ended, path=path
        )
        self._episodes = [C["decode"](EpisodeResult, e) for e in recording["episodes"]]
        self._evals = [C["decode"](EvalPoint, e) for e in recording["evals"]]
        self._checkpoints = [
            dataclasses.replace(c, run=name, path=path / "checkpoints" / c.path.name)
            for c in (C["decode"](Checkpoint, c) for c in recording["checkpoints"])
        ]

    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]:
        return list(self._episodes)

    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]:
        return list(self._evals)

    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]:
        return list(self._checkpoints)


def double(seed: int = 1, status: str = "finished") -> ReplayRun:
    return ReplayRun(C["recording"]("runlog.Run"), seed, status)

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/runlog.RunWriter/double.py
"""Replay double of `runlog.RunWriter`: the writes of a finished 3-checkpoint snake run.

Built with the recorded root, config and spec, it writes no files: it keeps every call in `calls`
(compare them with `recorded_calls`) and returns the recorded `Checkpoint` for each `pct` and the
recorded `RunRecord` from `close`. `existing=True` stands for a run folder that already exists:
the constructor raises `RunExistsError` before anything is kept.
"""

import runpy
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, WorldSpec
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Failure, RunExistsError, RunRecord
from rllab.train import RunConfig

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


class ReplayRunWriter:
    def __init__(
        self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec, *, existing: bool = False
    ) -> None:
        recording = C["recording"]("runlog.RunWriter")
        self.recorded_calls: list[dict] = recording["calls"]
        path = Path(root) / config.world / config.variant / config.name
        if existing:
            raise RunExistsError(path)
        self._checkpoints = {
            c["pct"]: C["decode"](Checkpoint, c["returns"])
            for c in self.recorded_calls
            if c["call"] == "checkpoint"
        }
        self._record = C["decode"](RunRecord, self.recorded_calls[-1]["returns"])
        self.path = path
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None:
        self.calls.append({"call": "episode", "result": C["encode"](EpisodeResult, result)})

    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None:
        self.calls.append({"call": "eval", "point": C["encode"](EvalPoint, point)})

    def checkpoint(
        self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]
    ) -> Checkpoint:
        self.calls.append({"call": "checkpoint", "pct": pct, "step": step, "state": dict(state)})
        return self._checkpoints[pct]

    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord:
        self.calls.append({"call": "close", "status": status, "failure": failure})
        return self._record


def double(*, existing: bool = False) -> ReplayRunWriter:
    recording = C["recording"]("runlog.RunWriter")
    config = C["decode"](RunConfig, recording["config"])
    spec = C["decode"](WorldSpec, recording["spec"])
    return ReplayRunWriter(Path(recording["root"]), config, spec, existing=existing)

"""Fake `core.play_episode`: the snake rule on seed 7, traced (`samples/seed7_traced.json`).

It serves that one recording: a team without a policy raises `KeyError` naming it, as the real
loop does; any other seed, or `trace=False`, raises `ValueError`.
"""

import runpy
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import Episode, Policy, World

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


def play_episode(
    world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False
) -> Episode:
    for team in world.spec.teams:
        if team.name not in policies:
            raise KeyError(f"no policy for team {team.name!r}")
    if seed != 7 or not trace:
        raise ValueError("the fake play_episode serves the traced seed-7 episode only")
    return C["sample"]("core.play_episode", "seed7_traced")

"""Fake `worlds.make`: `make("snake")` is `core.World`'s recorded seed-42 snake double.

Any other world or variant, or an override, raises `ValueError` naming it, as the real factory
does for one it doesn't know.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import World

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


def make(world: str, variant: str = "default", **overrides: int | float) -> World:
    if world != "snake":
        raise ValueError(f"unknown world {world!r} (the fake serves snake only)")
    if variant != "default":
        raise ValueError(f"unknown variant {variant!r} of snake")
    if overrides:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown override {sorted(overrides)[0]!r} (the fake takes none)")
    return C["double"]("core.World")

"""Fake `methods.make_learner`: the recorded DQN learner double (`methods.Learner`).

`dqn` needs a `Discrete` action space; a mismatch raises `ValueError` naming the method and the
action format, as the real factory does. `ppo` isn't built in M1, so the fake refuses it too.
"""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Discrete, WorldSpec
from rllab.methods import Learner, MethodConfig

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


def make_learner(
    config: MethodConfig, spec: WorldSpec, team: str, rng: np.random.Generator
) -> Learner:
    if config.method != "dqn":
        raise ValueError(f"the fake builds dqn only, not {config.method!r}")
    if not isinstance(spec.action, Discrete):
        raise ValueError(f"method 'dqn' needs a Discrete action space, not {spec.action!r}")
    return C["double"]("methods.Learner")

"""Fake `methods.load_policy`: the snake rule's replay double for any snake checkpoint.

A checkpoint whose spec differs from `spec` raises `SpecMismatchError` naming each differing
field, as the real loader does.
"""

import dataclasses
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import Policy, WorldSpec
from rllab.methods import SpecMismatchError
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


def load_policy(ckpt: Checkpoint, team: str, spec: WorldSpec) -> Policy:
    differing = tuple(
        f.name
        for f in dataclasses.fields(WorldSpec)
        if getattr(ckpt.spec, f.name) != getattr(spec, f.name)
    )
    if differing:
        raise SpecMismatchError(differing)
    if spec.world != "snake" or team not in {t.name for t in spec.teams}:
        raise ValueError(f"the fake serves snake checkpoints only, not {spec.world!r}/{team!r}")
    return C["double"]("core.Policy")

"""Fake `train.load_run_configs`: snake configs for seeds 1 and 2 (`samples/snake_s1_s2.json`).

Each is named `<file stem>-s<seed>`, as the real loader names them. A missing file raises
`FileNotFoundError`; a seed other than 1 or 2 raises `ValueError` (not in the recording).
"""

import dataclasses
import runpy
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.train import RunConfig

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


def load_run_configs(path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int]) -> list[RunConfig]:
    if not Path(path).is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no config at {path}")
    recorded = {c.seed: c for c in C["sample"]("train.load_run_configs", "snake_s1_s2")}
    unknown = [s for s in seeds if s not in recorded]
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"the fake has configs for seeds 1 and 2 only, not {unknown}")
    return [dataclasses.replace(recorded[s], name=f"{Path(path).stem}-s{s}") for s in seeds]

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/train.run_train/fake.py
"""Fake `train.run_train`: writes the finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder of `runlog.Run`.

Under `root/<world>/<variant>/<config.name>/` it writes `run.json` (the `RunRecord`, JSON form),
`episodes.jsonl` and `evals.jsonl` (one `EpisodeResult` or `EvalPoint` per line) and
`checkpoints/ckpt_<pct>.pt` (`torch.save` of each `Checkpoint`'s JSON form, loadable with
`weights_only=True`), then returns the record. An existing folder raises `RunExistsError` before
anything is written. The logs are the recording's, whatever the config's budget.
"""

import dataclasses
import json
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import torch

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult
from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, RunExistsError, RunRecord
from rllab.train import RunConfig

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


def _jsonl(path: Path, tp: type, items: list) -> None:
    path.write_text("".join(json.dumps(C["encode"](tp, i)) + "\n" for i in items), "utf-8")


def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord:
    path = Path(root) / config.world / config.variant / config.name
    if path.exists():
        raise RunExistsError(path)
    run = C["double"]("runlog.Run")
    record = dataclasses.replace(run.record, config=config, path=path)
    (path / "checkpoints").mkdir(parents=True)
    _jsonl(path / "episodes.jsonl", EpisodeResult, run.episodes())
    _jsonl(path / "evals.jsonl", EvalPoint, run.evals())
    for ckpt in run.checkpoints():
        saved = dataclasses.replace(ckpt, run=config.name, path=path / "checkpoints" / ckpt.path.name)
        torch.save(C["encode"](Checkpoint, saved), saved.path)
    (path / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(C["encode"](RunRecord, record), indent=1), "utf-8")
    return record

"""Fake `runlog.find_runs`: 5 finished snake runs (seeds 1-5), plus the incomplete
`snake-default-s6` when `include` names it; all are `runlog.Run` replay doubles.

Any other world or variant has no runs.
"""

import runpy
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.runlog import Run

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts.py"))


def find_runs(root: Path, world: str, variant: str, *, include: Sequence[str] = ()) -> list[Run]:
    if (world, variant) != ("snake", "default"):
        return []
    runs = [C["double"]("runlog.Run", seed) for seed in range(1, 6)]
    if "snake-default-s6" in include:
        runs.append(C["double"]("runlog.Run", 6, "incomplete"))
    return runs

"""Fake `charts.learning_curve`: writes a placeholder 1 x 1 PNG for the runs and returns its
`core.Output` (kind `chart`, made from the run names).

The file is `out/curve-<world>-<variant>.png` (or `curve-<world>-<variant>-<measure>.png`).
"""

import base64
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.baselines import BaselineMeans
from rllab.core import Output
from rllab.runlog import Run

PNG = base64.b64decode(
    "iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mNkYPhfDwAChwGA60e6kgAAAABJRU5ErkJggg=="
)


def learning_curve(
    runs: Sequence[Run], baselines: BaselineMeans, out: Path, *, measure: str | None = None
) -> Output:
    suffix = f"-{measure}" if measure else ""
    path = Path(out) / f"curve-{baselines.world}-{baselines.variant}{suffix}.png"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_bytes(PNG)
    return Output(path, "chart", tuple(r.record.config.name for r in runs))

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

    runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None
) -> list[Summary]:
    summary = C["sample"]("evaluate.Summary", "snake_pass")
    Path(out).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (Path(out) / "summary.json").write_text(
        json.dumps([C["encode"](Summary, summary)], indent=1), "utf-8"
    )
    return [summary]

U01.S146Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/test_samples.py (120 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/test_samples.py
"""Every contract sample validates against its schema, and each schema is the frozen type's."""

import json
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import EVAL_SEEDS, FINAL_SEEDS, Discrete

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parent / "contracts.py"))
SAMPLES = [(symbol, case) for symbol in C["TYPES"] for case in C["cases"](symbol)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", sorted(C["TYPES"]))
def test_schema_is_the_frozen_types(symbol):
    schema = C["read_json"](C["HERE"] / symbol / "schema.json")
    assert schema == C["schema_for"](C["TYPES"][symbol]), f"{symbol}'s type no longer matches"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", sorted(C["TYPES"]))
def test_each_type_has_a_sample(symbol):
    assert C["cases"](symbol)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("symbol", "case"), SAMPLES)
def test_sample_validates_against_its_schema(symbol, case):
    schema = C["read_json"](C["HERE"] / symbol / "schema.json")
    assert C["validate"](C["raw"](symbol, case), schema) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("symbol", "case"), SAMPLES)
def test_sample_decodes_to_its_type_and_back(symbol, case):
    tp = C["TYPES"][symbol]
    value = C["sample"](symbol, case)
    again = C["encode"](tp, value)
    assert json.dumps(again, sort_keys=True) == json.dumps(C["raw"](symbol, case), sort_keys=True)


def test_validate_reports_a_wrong_sample():
    schema = C["schema_for"](C["TYPES"]["train.CheckResult"])
    wrong = {"method": "dqn", "world": "check-discrete", "agreement": "high"}
    errors = C["validate"](wrong, schema)
    assert "$: missing 'passed'" in errors
    assert any(e.startswith("$.agreement") for e in errors)


def test_seed_sets_match_their_samples():
    assert C["sample"]("core.EVAL_SEEDS", "seeds") == EVAL_SEEDS
    assert C["sample"]("core.FINAL_SEEDS", "seeds") == FINAL_SEEDS


def test_seed_sets_are_disjoint_and_held_out():
    assert len(EVAL_SEEDS) == 20
    assert len(FINAL_SEEDS) == 100
    assert not set(EVAL_SEEDS) & set(FINAL_SEEDS)
    assert min(EVAL_SEEDS + FINAL_SEEDS) >= 1_000_000


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", C["cases"]("core.WorldSpec"))
def test_world_spec_teams_are_disjoint_and_cover_every_agent(case):
    spec = C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", case)
    agents = [a for team in spec.teams for a in team.agents]
    assert sorted(agents) == list(range(len(spec.agents)))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", C["cases"]("core.Step"))
def test_step_arrays_have_one_row_per_agent_and_inactive_rows_are_zero(case):
    step = C["sample"]("core.Step", case)
    assert step.obs.dtype == np.float32
    assert step.rewards.dtype == np.float32
    assert step.active.dtype == np.bool_
    assert step.obs.shape[0] == step.rewards.shape[0] == step.active.shape[0]
    assert not step.obs[~step.active].any()
    assert not step.rewards[~step.active].any()


def test_snake_step_has_snake_obs_len():
    spec = C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "snake")
    assert C["sample"]("core.Step", "snake_mid").obs.shape == (1, spec.obs_len)
    assert spec.action == Discrete(3)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", C["cases"]("core.Episode"))
def test_traced_episode_has_length_plus_one_frames(case):
    episode = C["sample"]("core.Episode", case)
    if episode.frames is not None:
        assert len(episode.frames) == episode.result.length + 1
        assert [f.step for f in episode.frames] == list(range(episode.result.length + 1))
        assert not episode.frames[0].rewards.any()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", C["cases"]("train.CheckResult"))
def test_check_result_passes_at_95_percent(case):
    result = C["sample"]("train.CheckResult", case)
    assert result.passed == (result.agreement >= 0.95)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", C["cases"]("runlog.Checkpoint"))
def test_checkpoint_pct_is_a_tenth(case):
    ckpt = C["sample"]("runlog.Checkpoint", case)
    assert ckpt.pct in range(0, 101, 10)
    assert set(ckpt.state) == {t.name for t in ckpt.spec.teams}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", C["cases"]("evaluate.Summary"))
def test_summary_with_fewer_than_5_runs_has_no_comparisons(case):
    summary = C["sample"]("evaluate.Summary", case)
    assert set(summary.run_means) == set(summary.runs)
    if len(summary.runs) < 5:
        assert summary.comparisons == ()
        assert summary.verdict.runs_needed == 5 - len(summary.runs)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", C["cases"]("runlog.RunRecord"))
def test_run_record_failure_only_when_failed(case):
    record = C["sample"]("runlog.RunRecord", case)
    assert (record.failure is not None) == (record.status == "failed")
    assert (record.ended is None) == (record.status in {"running", "incomplete"})

"""Each Protocol's or class's replay double serves its recording."""

import re
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import ActionError, Box, Discrete, EpisodeResult, Step
from rllab.methods import NonFiniteError, Transition
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parent / "contracts.py"))


def decode_step(data):
    return C["decode"](Step, data)


def assert_same_step(a, b):
    assert np.array_equal(a.obs, b.obs)
    assert np.array_equal(a.rewards, b.rewards)
    assert np.array_equal(a.active, b.active)
    assert (a.done, a.truncated) == (b.done, b.truncated)


def test_world_double_replays_the_seed_42_snake_episode():
    world, rec = C["double"]("core.World"), C["recording"]("core.World")
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    assert rec["seed"] == world.recorded_seed == 42
    assert_same_step(world.reset(42), decode_step(rec["reset"]))
    steps = [world.step(a) for a in world.recorded_actions]
    for got, want in zip(steps, rec["steps"], strict=True):
        assert_same_step(got, decode_step(want["step"]))
    assert steps[-1].done
    assert steps[-1].truncated
    assert len(steps) == world.spec.max_steps == 5
    assert world.result() == C["decode"](EpisodeResult, rec["result"])
    assert len(world.draw().objects) == 5  # head, 3 body cells, food


def test_world_double_refuses_bad_actions_and_steps_after_done():
    world = C["double"]("core.World")
    world.reset(42)
    with pytest.raises(ActionError, match="snake"):
        world.step(np.array([3]))
    for action in world.recorded_actions:
        world.step(action)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="after done"):
        world.step(np.array([0]))


def test_world_double_restarts_on_any_seed():
    world = C["double"]("core.World")
    first = world.reset(42)
    again = world.reset(1_000_003)
    assert_same_step(first, again)
    assert world.seeds == [42, 1_000_003]


def test_policy_double_serves_the_snake_rule_recording():
    policy, rec = C["double"]("core.Policy"), C["recording"]("core.Policy")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    for call in rec["calls"]:
        obs = C["decode"](np.ndarray, call["obs"])
        action = policy.act(obs, rng)
        assert np.array_equal(action, C["decode"](np.ndarray, call["action"]))
        assert action.shape == (obs.shape[0],)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not in the recording"):
        policy.act(np.zeros((1, 12), dtype=np.float32), rng)


def test_policy_double_matches_the_world_double_actions():
    world, policy = C["double"]("core.World"), C["double"]("core.Policy")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    step = world.reset(42)
    for want in world.recorded_actions:
        action = policy.act(step.obs, rng)
        assert np.array_equal(action, want)
        step = world.step(action)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("case", "best"), [("corridor", [0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1]), ("point", None)])
def test_check_world_double_lists_states_and_best_actions(case, best):
    world = C["double"]("worlds.CheckWorld", case)
    states, actions = world.states(), world.best_actions()
    assert states.shape == (len(actions), world.spec.obs_len)
    if best is not None:
        assert actions.tolist() == best
    else:  # the best push is the sign of the offset to the target
        assert np.array_equal(actions[:, 0], -np.sign(states[:, 0]))
    step = world.reset(3)
    while not step.done:
        step = world.step(world.recorded_actions[len(world.actions)])
    assert world.result().length == len(world.actions)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["snake", "fish"])
def test_random_policy_double_stays_inside_the_action_space(case):
    policy = C["double"]("baselines.RandomPolicy", case)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(policy.rng_seed)
    actions = [policy.act(policy.obs, rng) for _ in range(5)]
    space = policy.action
    for a in actions:
        if isinstance(space, Discrete):
            assert a.shape == (1,)
            assert 0 <= a[0] < space.n
        else:
            assert isinstance(space, Box)
            assert a.shape == (1, space.dims)
            assert np.all((a >= space.low) & (a <= space.high))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="5 actions only"):
        policy.act(policy.obs, rng)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["snake", "fish_no_ball"])
def test_rule_policy_double_has_a_one_sentence_description(case):
    policy = C["double"]("baselines.RulePolicy", case)
    assert re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z][^.]*\.", policy.description)
    call = C["recording"]("baselines.RulePolicy")[case]["calls"][0]
    action = policy.act(C["decode"](np.ndarray, call["obs"]), np.random.default_rng(policy.rng_seed))
    assert np.array_equal(action, C["decode"](np.ndarray, call["action"]))


def test_learner_double_replays_10_dqn_transitions():
    learner, rec = C["double"]("methods.Learner"), C["recording"]("methods.Learner")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rec["rng_seed"])
    frozen = learner.policy()
    for i, step in enumerate(rec["steps"]):
        action = learner.act(C["decode"](np.ndarray, step["obs"]), rng)
        assert np.array_equal(action, C["decode"](np.ndarray, step["action"]))
        learner.observe(C["decode"](Transition, step["transition"]))
        assert learner.state_dict() == step["state"]
        assert len(learner.observed) == i + 1
    obs = C["decode"](np.ndarray, rec["policy_calls"][0]["obs"])
    assert np.array_equal(frozen.act(obs, rng), learner.policy().act(obs, rng))


def test_learner_double_transitions_follow_the_world_double():
    world, learner = C["double"]("core.World"), C["double"]("methods.Learner")
    first = learner.recorded_transitions[0]
    assert np.array_equal(first.obs, world.reset(42).obs)
    assert np.array_equal(first.next_obs, world.step(first.actions).obs)


def test_learner_double_raises_non_finite_before_changing_state():
    learner = C["double"]("methods.Learner", 2)
    t = learner.recorded_transitions[0]
    learner.observe(t)
    before = learner.state_dict()
    with pytest.raises(NonFiniteError, match="nan"):
        learner.observe(t)
    assert learner.state_dict() == before


def test_run_double_is_a_finished_3_checkpoint_snake_run():
    run = C["double"]("runlog.Run")
    assert run.record.status == "finished"
    assert run.record.config.name == "snake-default-s1"
    assert [p.step for p in run.evals()] == list(range(0, 2001, 100))
    assert [c.pct for c in run.checkpoints()] == [0, 50, 100]
    assert len(run.episodes()) == 12


def test_run_double_renames_per_seed_and_status():
    run = C["double"]("runlog.Run", 6, "incomplete")
    assert run.record.config.name == run.record.path.name == "snake-default-s6"
    assert run.record.status == "incomplete"
    assert run.record.ended is None
    assert all(c.run == "snake-default-s6" for c in run.checkpoints())


def test_run_writer_double_returns_the_recorded_writes():
    writer, rec = C["double"]("runlog.RunWriter"), C["recording"]("runlog.RunWriter")
    for call in rec["calls"]:
        if call["call"] == "checkpoint":
            ckpt = writer.checkpoint(call["pct"], call["step"], call["state"])
            assert ckpt.pct == call["pct"]
        elif call["call"] == "close":
            assert writer.close(call["status"]).status == "finished"
    assert [c["call"] for c in writer.calls] == ["checkpoint"] * 3 + ["close"]
    assert writer.path == Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1")


def test_run_writer_double_refuses_an_existing_run():
    with pytest.raises(RunExistsError, match="snake-default-s1"):
        C["double"]("runlog.RunWriter", existing=True)

"""Each boundary function's fake has the real signature and serves its fixtures."""

import inspect
import json
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
import torch

from rllab import baselines, charts, core, evaluate, methods, runlog, train, worlds
from rllab.core import Episode, Output
from rllab.methods import SpecMismatchError
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parent / "contracts.py"))
REAL = {
    "core.play_episode": core.play_episode,
    "worlds.make": worlds.make,
    "baselines.rule_for": baselines.rule_for,
    "baselines.baseline_means": baselines.baseline_means,
    "methods.make_learner": methods.make_learner,
    "methods.load_policy": methods.load_policy,
    "train.load_run_configs": train.load_run_configs,
    "train.run_check": train.run_check,
    "train.run_train": train.run_train,
    "train.run_batch": train.run_batch,
    "runlog.find_runs": runlog.find_runs,
    "runlog.open_run": runlog.open_run,
    "runlog.progress": runlog.progress,
    "charts.learning_curve": charts.learning_curve,
    "evaluate.final_evaluation": evaluate.final_evaluation,
}


def shape(fn):
    return [(p.name, p.kind, p.default) for p in inspect.signature(fn).parameters.values()]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", sorted(REAL))
def test_fake_has_the_real_signature(symbol):
    assert shape(C["fake"](symbol)) == shape(REAL[symbol])


def test_play_episode_fake_serves_the_traced_seed_7_episode():
    play = C["fake"]("core.play_episode")
    world, rule = C["double"]("core.World"), C["double"]("core.Policy")
    episode = play(world, {"snake": rule}, 7, trace=True)
    assert isinstance(episode, Episode)
    assert episode.result.seed == 7
    assert len(episode.frames) == episode.result.length + 1
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="snake"):
        play(world, {}, 7, trace=True)


def test_make_fake_serves_the_snake_double():
    make = C["fake"]("worlds.make")
    assert make("snake").spec.world == "snake"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fish"):
        make("fish", "one")


def test_rule_for_fake_serves_the_snake_rule():
    rule_for = C["fake"]("baselines.rule_for")
    assert rule_for("snake", "default", "snake").description.endswith(".")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="prey"):
        rule_for("prey", "default", "prey")


def test_baseline_means_fake_serves_snake():
    means = C["fake"]("baselines.baseline_means")("snake", "default")
    assert means == C["sample"]("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")


def test_make_learner_fake_serves_the_dqn_double_and_rejects_box():
    make_learner = C["fake"]("methods.make_learner")
    dqn = C["sample"]("methods.MethodConfig", "snake_dqn_defaults")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    learner = make_learner(dqn, C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "snake"), "snake", rng)
    assert learner.config == dqn
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="dqn"):
        make_learner(dqn, C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "fish_local"), "fish", rng)


def test_load_policy_fake_checks_the_spec():
    load_policy = C["fake"]("methods.load_policy")
    ckpt = C["sample"]("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_100pct")
    policy = load_policy(ckpt, "snake", ckpt.spec)
    call = C["recording"]("core.Policy")["calls"][0]
    obs = C["decode"](np.ndarray, call["obs"])
    assert np.array_equal(policy.act(obs, np.random.default_rng(0)), C["decode"](np.ndarray, call["action"]))
    with pytest.raises(SpecMismatchError, match="obs_len"):
        load_policy(ckpt, "snake", C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "fish_local"))


def test_load_run_configs_fake_names_configs_by_stem(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "snake-default.toml"
    path.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    configs = C["fake"]("train.load_run_configs")(path, [1, 2])
    assert [c.name for c in configs] == ["snake-default-s1", "snake-default-s2"]
    assert [c.seed for c in configs] == [1, 2]
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="missing"):
        C["fake"]("train.load_run_configs")(tmp_path / "missing.toml", [1])


def test_run_check_fake_serves_the_passing_dqn_check(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "snake-default.toml"
    path.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (result,) = C["fake"]("train.run_check")(path)
    assert (result.method, result.passed) == ("dqn", True)


def test_run_train_fake_writes_the_run_folder(tmp_path):
    run_train = C["fake"]("train.run_train")
    config = C["sample"]("train.RunConfig", "snake")
    record = run_train(config, tmp_path)
    assert record.status == "finished"
    assert record.path == tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / config.name
    lines = (record.path / "evals.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    assert len(lines) == 21
    saved = json.loads((record.path / "run.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert saved["status"] == "finished"
    ckpt = torch.load(record.path / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_100.pt", weights_only=True)
    assert (ckpt["pct"], ckpt["run"]) == (100, config.name)
    with pytest.raises(RunExistsError, match=config.name):
        run_train(config, tmp_path)


def test_run_batch_fake_returns_two_finished_records(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "snake-default.toml"
    path.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    records = C["fake"]("train.run_batch")(path, [1, 2], tmp_path / "runs")
    assert [r.config.name for r in records] == ["snake-default-s1", "snake-default-s2"]
    assert all(r.status == "finished" for r in records)


def test_find_runs_fake_serves_5_finished_plus_a_named_incomplete():
    find_runs = C["fake"]("runlog.find_runs")
    assert [r.record.status for r in find_runs(Path("runs"), "snake", "default")] == ["finished"] * 5
    runs = find_runs(Path("runs"), "snake", "default", include=["snake-default-s6"])
    assert runs[-1].record.status == "incomplete"
    assert find_runs(Path("runs"), "fish", "one") == []


def test_open_run_fake_serves_the_finished_run():
    run = C["fake"]("runlog.open_run")(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1"))
    assert run.record.status == "finished"
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="nowhere"):
        C["fake"]("runlog.open_run")(Path("runs/nowhere"))


def test_progress_fake_serves_the_35_percent_snapshot():
    snapshot = C["fake"]("runlog.progress")(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1"))
    assert snapshot.step / snapshot.budget == 0.35
    assert snapshot.latest.step == snapshot.step


def test_learning_curve_fake_writes_a_png(tmp_path):
    runs = C["fake"]("runlog.find_runs")(Path("runs"), "snake", "default")
    means = C["sample"]("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")
    output = C["fake"]("charts.learning_curve")(runs, means, tmp_path)
    assert isinstance(output, Output)
    assert output.kind == "chart"
    assert output.path.read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG")
    assert len(output.made_from) == 5


def test_final_evaluation_fake_serves_the_passing_summary(tmp_path):
    runs = C["fake"]("runlog.find_runs")(Path("runs"), "snake", "default")
    (summary,) = C["fake"]("evaluate.final_evaluation")(runs, tmp_path)
    assert summary.verdict.beats_baselines
    assert json.loads((tmp_path / "summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[0]["team"] == "snake"

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/tests/contracts/test_exports.py
"""Each component exports its contract, and `rllab` dispatches each M1 trigger to its entry."""

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from rllab import baselines, charts, cli, core, evaluate, methods, runlog, train, worlds
from rllab.cli import __main__ as rllab_main

EXPORTS = {
    core: {
        "EVAL_SEEDS", "FINAL_SEEDS", "ActionError", "ActionSpace", "Array", "Box", "Direction",
        "Discrete", "DrawState", "Drawable", "Episode", "EpisodeResult", "Frame", "MeasureSpec",
        "Output", "Policy", "Settings", "Step", "TeamSpec", "World", "WorldSpec", "play_episode",
    },
    worlds: {"CheckWorld", "make"},
    baselines: {"BaselineMeans", "RandomPolicy", "RulePolicy", "baseline_means", "rule_for"},
    methods: {
        "Learner", "MethodConfig", "NonFiniteError", "SpecMismatchError", "Transition",
        "load_policy", "make_learner", "method_defaults",
    },
    train: {
        "CheckFailedError", "CheckResult", "RunConfig", "load_run_configs", "run_batch",
        "run_check", "run_train",
    },
    runlog: {
        "Checkpoint", "EvalPoint", "Failure", "Progress", "Run", "RunExistsError", "RunRecord",
        "RunWriter", "find_runs", "open_run", "progress",
    },
    charts: {"MixedRunsError", "learning_curve"},
    evaluate: {"Comparison", "Summary", "Verdict", "compare", "final_evaluation"},
    cli: {"main"},
}  # fmt: skip


@pytest.mark.parametrize("module", EXPORTS, ids=lambda m: m.__name__)
def test_component_exports_its_contract(module):
    assert set(module.__all__) == EXPORTS[module]
    assert all(hasattr(module, name) for name in module.__all__)


DISPATCH = [
    (["check", "c.toml", "--seed", "3"], "run_check", ((Path("c.toml"), 3), {})),
    (
        ["train", "c.toml", "--seed", "4"],
        "run_batch",
        ((Path("c.toml"), [4], Path("runs")), {"check_seed": 0}),
    ),
    (
        ["--root", "r", "batch", "c.toml", "--seeds", "1-3,7", "--workers", "2"],
        "run_batch",
        ((Path("c.toml"), [1, 2, 3, 7], Path("r")), {"workers": 2, "check_seed": 0}),
    ),
    (["watch", "runs/snake/default/a"], "run_watch", ((Path("runs/snake/default/a"),), {})),
    (
        ["chart", "snake", "default", "--include", "a", "--measure", "length"],
        "run_chart",
        ((Path("runs"), "snake", "default"), {"include": ["a"], "measure": "length"}),
    ),
    (
        ["evaluate", "snake", "default", "--workers", "3"],
        "run_evaluate",
        ((Path("runs"), "snake", "default"), {"include": [], "workers": 3}),
    ),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("argv", "entry", "call"), DISPATCH, ids=[d[0][0] for d in DISPATCH])
def test_main_dispatches_each_trigger_to_its_entry(monkeypatch, capsys, argv, entry, call):
    calls = []

    def fake_entry(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append((args, kwargs))
        return ["first", "second"]

    monkeypatch.setattr(rllab_main, entry, fake_entry)
    assert cli.main(argv) == 0
    assert calls == [call]
    assert capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines() == ["first", "second"]


def test_main_prints_an_error_as_one_line_and_exits_1(monkeypatch, capsys):
    def failing(*_args, **_kwargs):
        raise FileNotFoundError("no config at c.toml")

    monkeypatch.setattr(rllab_main, "run_check", failing)
    assert cli.main(["check", "c.toml"]) == 1
    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    assert err == "rllab check: FileNotFoundError: no config at c.toml\n"

    assert dqn == dict(C_SAMPLE["settings"])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sarsa"):
        methods.method_defaults("sarsa")

U01.S165Read/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ac28a5666649e518e/scripts/hooks/stop_gate.sh
